Component-wise diagnostics and convergence results support the role of persistent heads and distillation-guided local optimization under label-skewed data.

</details>


### [97] [Same Flow, Different Paths: Variance Reduction in Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17287)
*Alexander Tyurin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从优化角度研究流匹配中路径选择对SGD收敛速度的影响，发现即使FM目标完全相同，不同路径也会显著改变收敛速度，并提出可数值求解的最优路径选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在流匹配中，速度模型依赖预定义的连接数据与噪声的路径，但路径选择通常被视为超参数而缺乏理论指导。作者希望从随机梯度方差与优化的角度理解路径如何影响训练效率。

Method: 将路径限制在诱导相同边缘分布与边缘速度场的等价类中，分析随机梯度方差。对线性速度模型和一维高斯数据推导SGD迭代复杂度的紧致上界并求解析最优线性路径；再将分析推广到一般FM问题，在固定θ下提出受约束的方差最小化问题PathOpt_θ，并给出可从样本估计的等价约束以便数值求解。

Result: 理论上发现路径选择可从根本上改变SGD收敛速度，即使FM目标完全一致；给出了一维高斯情形下最优线性路径的解析解，并证明路径约束对降方差至关重要——无约束降方差反而可能减慢收敛。实验在高斯数据、高斯混合模型和真实数据集上验证了理论结论。

Conclusion: 路径选择是影响流匹配训练效率的关键因素，应在保持相同FM问题的约束下通过最小化梯度方差来选取路径，本文提供了理论依据与可实现的数值方法。

Abstract: In flow matching (FM), a velocity model $v_θ$ is trained using a predefined path $g_t$ that connects data and noise samples (e.g., $g_t(x_0, x_1) = (1 - t) x_0 + t x_1$). In this work, we study the choice of this path from an optimization perspective by analyzing the variance of stochastic gradients. We consider the class $G(p_t,v^\star_t)$ of paths that induce the same marginal distributions $p_t$ and marginal velocity field $v^\star_t$, and therefore the same FM objective. Our main finding is that the choice of path $g_t$ can fundamentally change the convergence rate of SGD, even when the FM objective remains exactly the same. (i) For a linear velocity model and one-dimensional Gaussian data, we derive a tight bound on the SGD iteration complexity up to logarithmic factors and find an analytically optimal path that minimizes this bound among linear paths inducing the same FM problem. (ii) We then extend the variance analysis to general FM problems and formulate path selection at a fixed $θ$ as the variance-minimization problem PathOpt$_θ$, constrained to $g_t\in G(p_t,v^\star_t)$. We show that this constraint is essential: reducing variance without it can lead to slower convergence. (iii) Since the constraint $g_t \in G(p_t,v^\star_t)$ cannot generally be verified directly, we derive an equivalent formulation with constraints that can be estimated from samples, allowing paths to be found numerically. Our theoretical results are supported by experiments with Gaussian data, Gaussian mixture models, and real datasets.

</details>


### [98] [Hybrid Variational Quantum Circuits for Multivariate Regression and High-Dimensional Data Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17358)
*Koffi Ognandon Ayena,Frédéric Holweck,Serge Iovleff,Amah S d'Almeida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合变分量子电路（HVQC），在VQC后加入经典仿射层，实现向量值回归，实验上匹配高斯过程回归并优于XGBoost和随机森林。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子电路（VQC）是经典优化下的参数化量子电路。作者希望扩展VQC能力，实现向量值回归，同时避免使用多个独立标量电路带来的线性开销。

Method: 提出HVQC，将VQC与经典仿射后测量层结合。理论上证明基础的一比特和两比特电路可通过数据重复上传和纠缠近似二次函数与乘积，为整体架构奠定基础。

Result: 在两个合成图像重建数据集和Friedman1基准（40,568个测试样本）上，HVQC匹配高斯过程回归，并优于XGBoost和随机森林。消融实验确认量子与经典组件均不可或缺，且特征映射在混合量子-经典模型中起核心作用。

Conclusion: HVQC是一种有效的混合量子-经典回归架构，兼顾向量输出效率与预测性能，并强调特征映射的关键地位。

Abstract: Variational quantum circuits (VQCs) are parameterized quantum circuits optimized classically. We propose a hybrid variational quantum circuit (HVQC) extending VQCs with a classical affine post-measurement layer, enabling vector-valued regression without the linear overhead of independent scalar circuits. Theoretically, we show that elementary one-and two-qubit circuits can approximate quadratic functions and products via data re-uploading and entanglement, providing the foundations of the full architecture. Experimentally, on two synthetic image reconstruction datasets and the Friedman1 benchmark (40,568 test samples), our HVQC matches Gaussian Process Regression and outperforms XGBoost and Random Forest. An ablation study confirms that both quantum and classical components are essential, and results highlight the central role of the feature map in hybrid quantum-classical models.

</details>


### [99] [Large Language Models Develop Belief State Geometry In-Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17376)
*Daniel Balcells,Andrew Jun Lee,Chirag Rastogi,Paul M. Riechers,Adam Shai,Xavier Poncini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）对开源大模型进行探针分析，发现信念状态可从残差流激活中线性解码，并具有功能相关性，表明上下文学习近似贝叶斯最优预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型展现出强大的上下文学习（ICL）能力，但支撑这种能力的内部表征机制仍不清楚，本文旨在从表征层面探究ICL是否近似于对上下文推断生成模型的最优贝叶斯预测。

Method: 在受控设定下，用HMM生成的数据提示大模型，并探测其对应信念状态（给定观测历史的隐状态后验分布）。选取40个具有非平凡信念结构的HMM和6个开源LLM，使用线性探针解码残差流激活，并通过子空间补丁与引导干预验证其功能相关性。

Result: 信念状态可从残差流激活中线性解码，峰值探针R²达0.83-0.99，分布于从早期到后期不同层；对探针识别的子空间进行干预后下游预测质量接近未干预模型，而对照组性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 研究提供了表征层面的证据，表明开源大模型的上下文学习近似于对上下文推断生成模型的最优贝叶斯预测，并将输入分布结构与激活几何关系的研究从玩具网络扩展到生产级大模型。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) trained on next-token prediction exhibit remarkable in-context learning (ICL) abilities, yet the representations that support ICL remain poorly understood. We consider such representations in a controlled setting: prompting LLMs with data emitted from hidden Markov models (HMMs) and probing for the corresponding belief state -- the posterior distribution over the HMM's hidden states given the observed token history. Across six open-source LLMs prompted with data from 40 HMMs selected for non-trivial belief structure, we find that belief states are linearly decodable from residual stream activations, with peak probe $R^2$-values from 0.83-0.99 across HMM and LLM combinations, ranging from early to late layers. To establish functional relevance, we intervene directly on the probe-identified subspace via patching and steering, resulting in downstream prediction quality on the order of the untampered model, while controls degrade performance substantially. Together, these results provide representation-level evidence that ICL in open-source LLMs approximates optimal Bayesian prediction over a context-inferred generative model. More broadly, our findings extend prior results linking input-distribution structure to activation geometry: from toy networks trained explicitly on HMM data to production-scale LLMs.

</details>


### [100] [OPEN-1B: A Fully Auditable Training Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17380)
*John Donaghy,Brian Wilcox,Oğuzhan Ersoy,Shikhar Rastogi,Adam St Arnaud,Alexey Titov,Jordan Greenberg,Ben Fielding,Harry Grieve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"完全可审计"的模型透明新层级：在异构商用硬件上以位级确定性独立复现训练中每一步操作，并通过集体验证方案让多个独立审计者分步认证整个训练过程，发布Open-1B及其全部训练资料与审计工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源语言模型存在可复现性问题：尽管公开了权重、数据和配方，但浮点运算的非结合性导致无法证明可复现。现有确定性执行模式只在同一机器上有效，无法跨硬件验证检查点是否真按声明配方训练，从而留下隐藏数据、注入偏见或后门空间，而proof-of-learning等方法无法排除这些风险。

Method: 对训练非确定性的来源施加确定顺序，包括GPU核归约、数据并行集群中的批次顺序以及节点间/节点内集合通信；从而可将大规模分布式训练的任意单步在单台商用硬件上重放并与公开轨迹比对；并设计集体验证方案，由多个独立审计者各自认证单步、共同覆盖整轮训练。

Result: 实现位级确定的跨硬件可复现：任意单步可在单台商用硬件上重放并核验；发布Open-1B模型，连同完整预训练数据集、每个中间检查点、训练代码库以及复现和验证任意训练步骤所需的审计工具。

Conclusion: 确立了"完全可审计"的模型透明新层级，使开源模型训练过程在异构商用硬件上具备位级可验证性，为排除隐藏数据、偏见和后门提供了可操作的技术路径。

Abstract: Open-source language models have a reproducibility problem. Despite releasing weights, training data, and recipes, none of them are provably reproducible due to the non-associativity of floating-point arithmetic. Deep learning frameworks often offer a deterministic execution mode, allowing reproducible operations on the same machines. Unfortunately, this determinism does not carry across hardware such that a user can verify that a released checkpoint was actually produced using the declared training recipe. This leaves room for undisclosed data, injected biases, or backdoors that existing techniques such as proof-of-learning or proof-of-training-data cannot rule out.
  We introduce a new tier of model transparency, fully auditable, in which every operation on every data sample during training is independently reproducible on heterogeneous commodity hardware with bitwise certainty. By imposing a definite order on the sources of training nondeterminism, GPU kernel reductions, data batch ordering across a data-parallel cluster, and inter/intra-node collective communication, we make it possible to replay any individual step of a large, distributed training run on a single piece of commodity hardware and check it against the published trajectory.
  Because replaying an entire run on one machine is infeasible, we support this with a collective verification scheme in which many independent auditors each certify individual steps, together covering the whole run. We release Open-1B, a model trained under this regime, together with its full pretraining dataset, every intermediate checkpoint, the training codebase, and the audit harness needed to reproduce and verify any step of its training.

</details>


### [101] [Bridging the Confidence Gap: Temperature Scaling for Calibrating Test-Time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17386)
*Yuwei Liang,Jian Liang,Dapeng Hu,Yinuo Xu,Ran He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoTS与E-CoTS两种事后校准方法，利用零样本预测良好校准的特性，在不损害甚至提升准确率的同时显著降低测试时提示调优（TPT）的校准误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时提示调优（TPT）虽能提升单实例适配准确率，却常损害校准性能；现有校准方法多通过额外正则项促进文本嵌入分散，虽降低校准误差，但往往导致准确率下降。

Method: 受零样本预测良好校准特性的启发，提出CoTS：对适配预测施加温度缩放，以最小化其与零样本预测之间的置信度差距。为充分利用适配中的多次增强，引入弱-强集成策略进一步提升准确率，并将CoTS应用于该集成得到E-CoTS以保持良好校准属性。

Result: 在多种数据集与骨干网络上验证有效。例如在ImageNet变体上，E-CoTS将TPT的平均期望校准误差（ECE）从11.90%降至5.38%，同时将准确率从60.74%提升至62.95%；与现有校准方法结合时通常能同时提升准确率与校准性能。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效缓解校准误差且不牺牲主任务准确率，甚至可提升准确率，并可与现有校准方法互补。

Abstract: Test-time prompt tuning (TPT) enables adaptation on a single test instance, achieving improved accuracy but often sacrificing calibration performance. Most existing calibration methods introduce additional regularization terms to promote dispersion across text embeddings and reduce calibration error, yet these methods often suffer from a drop in accuracy. Motivated by the well-calibrated nature of zero-shot predictions, we propose CoTS, a simple yet effective post-hoc calibration method that preserves accuracy. Specifically, CoTS applies temperature scaling to minimize the confidence gap between adapted and zero-shot predictions. To fully exploit the potential of multiple augmentations during adaptation, we introduce a weak-strong ensemble strategy that further boosts accuracy. We then apply CoTS to this ensemble, termed E-CoTS, to maintain its well-calibrated property. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets and backbones show that our approaches effectively mitigate miscalibration without compromising primary accuracy. For instance, E-CoTS reduces the average expected calibration error of TPT from 11.90% to 5.38% on ImageNet variants, while even increasing accuracy from 60.74% to 62.95%. Moreover, when integrated with existing calibration methods, E-CoTS usually enhances both accuracy and calibration simultaneously.

</details>


### [102] [Knowledge as Orbit: Finite Collections as Phases of an Exactly Periodic Latent Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17417)
*Siddharth Pal,Viktoria Rojkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将有限知识以"内蕴"方式存储为一条紧凑循环律（学习到的旋转轨道），用共享解码器解码各相位，R^X=单位矩阵保证精确闭合；在图像与循环视频上验证其折叠无缝、参数匹配下保真度不劣。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限知识通常以外延方式存储，每个条目一个编码或向量，存储开销随条目数线性增长。作者追问：有限集合能否改为内蕴存储——即由一条返回起点的紧凑律所解码出的轨道。动机在于用动力学与代数约束替代独立实例的堆叠，从而降低潜在存储并保证精确循环性。

Method: 将第i个条目编码为学习潜空间中固定旋转的第i个相位，所有相位由共享网络解码。潜变量经由整数谐波（循环的真实离散傅里叶算子）上的旋转组推进，使R^X等于单位矩阵，精确闭合由代数保证而非学习得到。图像作为可控载体，循环视频则是相位顺序即内容自身时间结构的场景。实验对比固定解码器下不同算子的表现：通用学习算子、保范但非周期算子、精确周期算子。

Result: 固定解码器时，通用学习算子发散，保范非周期算子在循环附近退化，精确周期算子表现平稳；在真实图像上差距更大。容量由解码器预算决定：稠密解码器每张清晰图像带来结构性开销，任何规模都无法与压缩相调和；小型卷积解码器在共享流形的对象上可同时达到清晰与压缩。码本对照显示生成律在重建意义上"免费"，却使潜存储成倍增长。在七个基准片段上，与匹配的帧索引基线相比，循环方法在等参数下达到相同或更好保真度并机械精度级无缝衔接，基线则留下可见接缝；将基线频率钉到循环谐波上其接缝也消失，确认精确周期性是关键约束。

Conclusion: 有限循环知识可以作为动力学而非独立实例来存储：精确递推由代数提供，内容由共享解码器提供。精确周期性是消除接缝、实现压缩存储的关键操作约束。

Abstract: Finite knowledge is usually stored extensionally, one code or vector per item. We ask whether a finite collection can instead be stored intensionally, as the decoded orbit of one compact law that returns exactly to its start. For X objects, we encode item i as the i-th phase of a fixed rotation in a learned latent space and decode all phases with a shared network; the latent advances through a bank of rotations at integer harmonics of the cycle, a real discrete Fourier operator, so that R^X equals the identity and exact closure is guaranteed rather than learned. Images are a controlled carrier; looping video is the case where the phase order is the content's own temporal structure. Holding the decoder fixed and varying only the operator, a general learned operator diverges, a norm-preserving but non-periodic one degrades around the loop, and the exactly periodic operator is flat; on real images the gap widens. Capacity is then the decoder's budget: dense decoders carry a structural overhead per crisp image that no size reconciles with compression, while a small convolutional decoder on objects that share a manifold reaches crisp and compressed. A codebook control shows the generative law is free in reconstruction terms while multiplying the latent store many-fold. On seven benchmark clips, against a matched frame-index baseline, the cycle reaches equal or better fidelity at equal parameters while wrapping at machine precision, where the baseline leaves a visible seam; pinning the baseline's frequencies to loop harmonics closes its seam too, confirming that exact periodicity is the operative constraint. Finite cyclic knowledge can be stored as dynamics rather than independent instances, with exact recurrence supplied by algebra and content by a shared decoder.

</details>


### [103] [Learning-Guided Planning in Large Dynamic Action Spaces: Budgeted Tree Search for One-to-Many Mobile Charging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17429)
*Liang-Ching Tao,Pi-Chung Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LP-BTS 是一种学习引导的规划架构，通过图提议策略、价值评论家和边预算 PUCT，在动态大规模充电停靠动作空间中实现更好的传感器存活表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多学习型序贯决策系统将当前状态直接映射到动作，但当候选动作数量众多、具有几何结构且随状态重建时，这种捷径变得脆弱。一对一移动充电场景即为例证：N=250 传感器时初始状态约产生 1,125 个候选停靠动作，每个停靠同时服务范围内传感器，且动作空间随传感器死亡而动态变化。

Method: 提出 LP-BTS：图提议策略集中小规模候选支撑集，学习价值评论家评估叶节点，边预算 PUCT 在提交动作前比较短模拟未来。策略无需固定输出头即可对动作集评分，单一冻结检查点覆盖 736–2,813 停靠的动作空间。

Result: 匹配消融显示：均匀采样损失 8.8 个存活百分点；固定目标支撑下，PUCT 联合保留 1.4 分（约 250 个传感器中的 3.5 个），直接策略选择多行驶 23%。在预设密封 30 场景确认集上一次性评估，LP-BTS 取得最高存活率 0.4545 和 alive-AUC 0.8031；相对最强领域工程比较器存活优势为 +0.0066（95% CI [-0.0037, +0.0184]），差异未定论；但在每个配对场景上超过截止时间启发式和两个源自直接策略重构的方法。

Conclusion: 在该设定下，结果为大规模动态动作空间中的学习引导规划提供了受控证据。

Abstract: Many learned sequential decision systems map the current state directly to an action. That shortcut becomes brittle when candidate actions are numerous, geometrically structured, and rebuilt with the state. One-to-many mobile charging makes this setting concrete: with N=250 sensors, the initial state induces about 1,125 candidate charging-stop actions; each chosen stop simultaneously serves its in-range sensors, and the action universe changes as sensors die. LP-BTS is a learning-guided planning architecture: a graph proposal policy concentrates a small candidate support, a learned value critic evaluates leaves, and edge-budgeted PUCT compares short simulated futures before committing an action. Because the policy scores this set without a fixed output head, a single frozen checkpoint covers every evaluated setting, spanning action universes from 736 to 2,813 stops. Matched ablations reveal complementary effects: uniform sampling costs 8.8 survival percentage points, while, with targeted support fixed, PUCT jointly retains 1.4 points (about 3.5 of 250 sensors) and direct policy selection travels 23% farther. On a prospectively specified, sealed 30-scenario confirmatory bank evaluated once, LP-BTS attains the highest observed survival (0.4545) and alive-AUC (0.8031). Its estimated survival advantage over the strongest domain-engineered comparator is +0.0066 (95% CI [-0.0037, +0.0184]), an unresolved difference, while it exceeds a deadline heuristic and two source-derived direct-policy reconstructions on every paired scenario. Both learned rows are trained, source-derived reconstructions of variants reported by Gong et al. In this setting, the results provide controlled evidence about learning-guided planning in a large, dynamic action space.

</details>


### [104] [Reduced-Space Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization of Process Simulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17440)
*Niki Triantafyllou,Andrea Bernardi,Maria M. Papathanasiou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RS-MFBO框架，结合全局敏感性分析与多保真高斯过程，显著减少高保真仿真次数，实现高维昂贵黑箱优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业流程模拟的严格仿真成本极高，且复杂设计空间存在维数灾难，传统优化方法计算上难以承受。

Method: 将全局敏感性分析（GSA）用于降维，结合保真度增强的高斯过程捕捉低保真近似与高保真评估之间的相关性；采用成本感知的采集策略，并辅以冷却与提升机制，自适应地在不同保真度间分配样本。

Result: 在SuperPro Designer的质粒DNA生物工艺和Aspen HYSYS的绿色燃料合成装置上验证；在多种经济与物理目标下，方法大幅减少了高保真仿真评估次数，同时保持与单保真基线相当的优化性能。

Conclusion: RS-MFBO是一种可扩展、与仿真器无关的方法，适用于成本受限的黑箱优化问题。

Abstract: Optimizing industrial process flowsheets is often computationally prohibitive due to the high cost of rigorous simulations and the curse of dimensionality inherent in complex design spaces. To address these challenges, we present a reduced-space multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (RS-MFBO) framework designed for high-dimensional, expensive black-box functions. The approach integrates Global Sensitivity Analysis (GSA) for dimensionality reduction with a fidelity-augmented Gaussian process that captures correlations between low-cost approximations and expensive high-fidelity evaluations. A cost-aware acquisition strategy, augmented with cooldown and promotion mechanisms, adaptively guides the allocation of samples across fidelities. The framework is validated on two distinct industrial process simulators: a plasmid DNA bioprocess in SuperPro Designer and a green fuel synthesis plant in Aspen HYSYS. Results across diverse economic and physical objectives demonstrate that the proposed method substantially reduces the number of high-fidelity simulator evaluations while maintaining competitive optimization performance compared to single-fidelity baselines. These results highlight RS-MFBO as a scalable, simulator-agnostic approach for cost-constrained black-box optimization.

</details>


### [105] [Coupled Calibration and Learning: Mitigating Teacher Bias in LLM Distillation without Target-Domain Reward Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17474)
*Haichen Hu,Yuheng Zhang,David Simchi-Levi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出耦合校准与学习（CCL）蒸馏算法，通过源问题奖励反馈校准教师并用其更新学生，学生反过来指导后续校准，证明学生到最优学生的KL散度多项式速率收敛到零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM蒸馏旨在将强教师能力迁移到小学生，但直接模仿会连教师的系统性偏差和错误一起转移；在协变量偏移下，教师对目标问题的可靠性未知且缺乏目标域奖励反馈，这一问题尤为突出。

Method: CCL通过token级分支将教师校准与学生更新耦合，仅使用源问题上的奖励反馈：每轮先用源反馈校准教师，再用校准后的教师训练学生在目标问题上的表现；更新后的学生反过来影响后续校准。在自回归策略框架下进行理论分析。

Result: 证明输出学生到oracle学生的期望平均KL散度随迭代次数以多项式速率收敛到零；oracle在学生类内最大化真实参考正则化目标奖励。分析量化了投影学生梯度更新的进展并控制了教师校准误差，同时建立与正则化直接匹配的分离：即使教师取得比所有学生策略更高的正则化目标奖励，直接匹配误差仍可能远离零。

Conclusion: 结果表明，LLM蒸馏可通过耦合校准与学习克服持续存在的教师偏差，在无目标域奖励反馈的情况下恢复最优学生。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) distillation aims to transfer the capabilities of a powerful teacher to a smaller student. Direct imitation, however, can also transfer the teacher's systematic bias and errors. This challenge is particularly pronounced under covariate shift, when the teacher's reliability on target questions is uncertain and target-domain reward feedback is unavailable. We propose Coupled Calibration and Learning (CCL), an LLM distillation algorithm that couples teacher calibration with student updates through token-level branching, using reward feedback only on source questions. Each iteration calibrates the teacher using source feedback and then uses the calibrated teacher to train the student on target questions. The updated student, in turn, informs subsequent calibration. In an autoregressive policy framework, we prove that the output student's expected average Kullback-Leibler divergence to the oracle student converges to zero at a polynomial rate in the number of iterations. The oracle maximizes the true reference-regularized target reward within the student class, which need not represent the unrestricted optimal policy. Our analysis quantifies the progress of projected student gradient updates while controlling the error in teacher calibration. We further establish a separation from regularized direct matching: its error relative to the oracle student can remain bounded away from zero even when the teacher achieves higher regularized target reward than every student policy. These results demonstrate that LLM distillation can overcome persistent teacher bias and recover the optimal student through coupled calibration and learning, without target-domain reward feedback.

</details>


### [106] [FreqSpaNet: Frequency and Spatial Learning of SFPF for Physical Layer Hardware Integrity Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17491)
*Xiaoxuan Huang,Jinlong Xu,YiZhe Wang,Meng Zhang,Xian Li,Yuying Bian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FreqSpaNet，用于开放集硬件异常检测的时空频极化指纹表示学习网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未经授权的硬件更换可在保持设备逻辑身份的同时改变物理实现，威胁硬件完整性验证；时空频极化指纹（SFPF）虽能刻画设备相关响应，但其频率与空间维度具有不同的结构依赖关系，需要针对性建模。

Method: FreqSpaNet 包含频率分支（捕捉相邻频率间的局部变化）与几何感知空间分支（利用角度信息建模方向关系），两路表示通过自适应融合结合，并采用互补预训练在保留频率与空间表示各自特性的同时捕捉共享信息。

Result: 平均 AUROC 达 96.31%，较基线高 9.05 个百分点；在七种硬件更换场景下验证了有效性。

Conclusion: FreqSpaNet 能有效学习 SFPF 表示，在开放集硬件异常检测任务中显著优于基线，并在多种硬件更换场景下保持稳健。

Abstract: Unauthorized hardware replacement can preserve a wireless device's logical identity while altering its physical implementation, posing a challenge to hardware integrity verification. Spatio-frequency polarization fingerprints (SFPFs) capture device-dependent responses across multiple frequencies and directions, but their frequency and spatial dimensions exhibit different structural dependencies. We propose FreqSpaNet, an SFPF representation learning network for open set hardware anomaly detection. A frequency branch captures local variations among neighboring frequencies, while a geometry-aware spatial branch models directional relationships using angular information. The two representations are combined through adaptive fusion, and complementary pretraining further captures shared information while preserving the distinct characteristics of the frequency and spatial representations. Experiments show that FreqSpaNet achieves a mean AUROC of 96.31\%, 9.05 points above the baseline. Results under seven hardware replacement scenarios further verify the effectiveness of FreqSpaNet.

</details>


### [107] [ENCP: Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction for Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17499)
*Vicky Feliren,A. Taufiq Asyhari,Muhamad Risqi U. Saputra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction (ENCP)，通过对非一致性分数按策略残差置信度重缩放并按episode校准，解决VLN中变长序列步间依赖导致标准CP覆盖率失效的问题，在R2R和REVERIE上实现每步覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN模型的不确定性估计对识别模糊、不可靠预测并使智能体做出更安全导航决策至关重要。作为最先进的不确定性估计框架之一，conformal prediction (CP) 在VLN中很有前景，但VLN智能体需要一系列步骤，标准CP校准无法在其承诺的依赖、变长VLN episode上提供覆盖保证。作者提出ENCP来解决该问题。

Method: 提出Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction (ENCP)：按策略的残差置信度对非一致性分数进行重缩放，并对每个episode校准一个最大分数。在可交换的校准与测试episode假设下，该构造在episode内步骤间允许依赖，同时以至少1-α的概率覆盖每一步的真值。

Result: 在R2R和REVERIE数据集上，对四种VLN策略和三种非一致性分数进行实验，ENCP在seen-to-unseen评估中满足所有报告的每步经验覆盖目标。

Conclusion: 结果表明ENCP能够提供模型无关的不确定性估计，可能有助于判断VLN智能体何时应转交给更强的预测器（包括人工协助）。

Abstract: Uncertainty estimation for Vision-Language-Navigation (VLN) models is a critical task since it can help identify ambiguous and unreliable predictions, enabling agents to make safer navigation decisions. As one of the most advanced uncertainty estimation frameworks, conformal prediction (CP) offers a promising approach for uncertainty estimation in VLN. However, given that VLN agent requires a sequence of steps, standard calibration in conformal prediction fails to provide coverage guarantee it promises over a dependent, variable-length VLN episode. To this end, we propose Episode-Normalized Conformal Prediction (ENCP), which rescales a nonconformity score by the policy's residual confidence and calibrates one maximum score per episode. Under exchangeable calibration and test episodes, this construction covers the ground truth at every step with probability at least $1 - α$, while allowing dependence among steps within an episode. Across four VLN policies and three nonconformity scores on R2R and REVERIE dataset, ENCP meets all reported empirical step-coverage targets on the seen-to-unseen evaluation. These results demonstrate that ENCP can provide model-agnostic uncertainty estimates, which might be useful for determining when a VLN agent should defer to a more capable predictor, including human assistance.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Novel Iterative Construction Methods for the Blocking Job Shop Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16007)
*Adel Dabah,Karima Rihane,Hocine Saadi,Andreas Herten,Farouk Benslimane,Mohammed Lamine Bahmani,Abdelhakim Aitzai*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对阻塞作业车间调度问题(BJSSP)，提出基于集束搜索的三种启发式算法BS-ICH、PMS-BS及GPU加速版G-PMS-BS，在标准算例上刷新大量最优解，最大实例实现44倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻塞作业车间调度问题广泛存在于现代复杂制造、生产、物流与服务场景中，相邻工序间不允许中间存储。该特性导致元启发式算法求解时可行解与探索解比例极低，搜索效率面临巨大挑战，因此需要高效的新方法。

Method: 提出三种集束搜索启发式：BS-ICH通过迭代扩展部分解构造可行调度，并维持宽度为k的集束以保留多个高质量部分解；PMS-BS为其CPU并行扩展，运行数百个实例并引入机器偏向的多样性以扩大搜索空间、跳出局部最优；G-PMS-BS为GPU加速变体，采用两阶段内核架构，将轻量打分与定向状态重构分离，可扩展至2000道工序实例。此外，混合CPU+GPU模式利用空闲主机核心并发探索，并采用负载均衡策略降低同步开销。

Result: G-PMS-BS相较CPU基线实现44倍加速。在全部标准Lawrence与Taillard算例上，G-PMS-BS为22个Lawrence基准和77个Taillard实例刷新了已知最优结果，最大100x20实例上makespan降幅达13%。

Conclusion: 基于集束搜索并结合CPU/GPU并行与多样性策略的方法能显著提升BJSSP求解的效率与质量，在大规模标准算例上验证了其优越性，刷新了多项已知最优解。

Abstract: The Blocking Job-Shop Scheduling Problem (BJSSP) arises in modern and complex manufacturing, production, logistics, and service where no intermediate storage is allowed between consecutive operations. This creates a significant challenge for meta-heuristics due to the low ratio of feasible to explored solutions when solving the problem. To address this problem efficiently, we propose three new beam-search-based heuristics: the Beam Search Iterative Construction Heuristic (BS-ICH), its CPU-parallel extension Parallel Multi-Strategy Beam Search (PMS-BS), and a GPU-accelerated variants G-PMS-BS. BS-ICH constructs feasible schedules by iteratively extending partial solutions, while maintaining a beam of width k to preserve multiple high-quality partial schedules. PMS-BS runs hundreds of parallel BS-ICH instances with machine-biased diversity to expand the search space and escape local optima. G-PMS-BS offloads the beam expansion onto massively parallel GPU hardware using a two-phase kernel architecture that separates lightweight scoring from targeted state reconstruction, enabling scaling to instances with 2,000 operations. A hybrid CPU+GPU mode further exploits idle host cores for concurrent exploration, using load-balancing strategy to minimize synchronization overhead. G-PMS-BS achieves a 44x speedup over the CPU baseline. Experiments on all standard Lawrence and Taillard instances demonstrate that G-PMS-BS establishes new best-known results for 22 Lawrence benchmarks and 77 Taillard instances, with makespan reductions of up to 13% on the largest 100x20 instances.

</details>


### [109] [Co-Skill: A Collaborative Communication Framework for Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16008)
*Yilin Ma,Yangi Pan,Weihao Yang,Peixin Zeng,Jiannan Xu,Hao Huang,Wen Xia*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出协作通信框架CCF，通过云端感知轨迹前缀树、边缘感知渐进技能树及协作技能演化，解决云端LLM与边缘SLM之间"盲目通信"问题，显著降低token消耗并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体通过技能演化迭代提升任务成功率至关重要。混合演化范式（云端LLM分析生成技能、边缘SLM执行内化技能）成本高效，但现有方法（如SkillRL）成功率低、token消耗高。作者发现根源在于"盲目通信"：云端无法感知边缘的执行能力，边缘也不理解云端的分析需求。

Method: 提出协作通信框架CCF，包含三项技术：(1)云端感知的前缀合并轨迹树，边缘通过合并共享前缀并定位分歧点压缩轨迹，便于云端高效分析；(2)边缘感知的渐进技能树，云端逐步构建层次化技能树以匹配边缘SLM执行能力；(3)基于这两棵树构建的协作技能演化方案，以分离方式演化云端LLM与边缘SLM，共同提升任务成功率。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上的实验表明，CCF相比最先进的混合方法减少LLM+SLM token消耗15.6%–41.9%，同时任务成功率持续提升25.8%–76.4%。

Conclusion: CCF通过建立云端与边缘之间的协作通信机制，有效克服了盲目通信导致的低成功率与高token开销问题，在降低成本的同时显著提升任务成功率，验证了协作通信在边缘-云混合演化中的有效性。

Abstract: Agent evolution through skills becomes critical for LLM-based agents to iteratively improve task success rate. Hybrid evolution is a cost-efficient paradigm where a cloud LLM analyzes and generates skills while an edge SLM executes and internalizes them. However, existing hybrid methods, such as SkillRL, still suffer from low success rate and high token usage. We find this stems from blind communication: the cloud cannot perceive the edge's execution capability, while the edge does not understand the cloud's analysis needs.
  We thus propose the Collaborative Communication Framework (CCF) to achieve effective edge-cloud evolution. CCF is realized via three techniques: (1) a cloud-aware prefix-merged trajectory trie where the edge compresses trajectories by merging shared prefixes and pinpointing divergence points for efficient cloud analysis, (2) an edge-aware progressive skill tree where the cloud progressively builds a hierarchical skill tree to match edge SLM execution capability, and (3) a collaborative skill evolution scheme upon these two trees that evolves cloud LLM and edge SLM in a separated way to jointly improve task success rate. Experiments across ALFWorld and WebShop show that CCF reduces LLM+SLM tokens by 15.6%--41.9% over state-of-the-art hybrid methods while consistently improving 25.8%--76.4% task success rate.

</details>


### [110] [Vectorization Of Narrow Matrix Multiplication for Ascend AI Inference Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16009)
*Anton Shurygin,Aleksandr Frolov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在华为昇腾NPU上优化矩阵乘法的新方法，利用AscendC向量指令将计算从Cube单元卸载到Vector单元，在MLA DeepSeek-V3算子上实现单token处理平均20%的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键洞察在于：在矩阵-向量乘法（窄矩阵乘法）过程中，Cube单元（AIC）经常未被充分利用，而Vector单元（AIV）在算子大部分运行时间内处于空闲状态。此外，尽管有官方文档和活跃的CANN社区，AscendC实用优化技术的文献仍存在明显空白。

Method: 提出MatMul算法，利用AscendC的向量指令，将计算从Cube单元有效卸载到Vector单元，从而实现AIV与AIC计算的相互重叠。

Result: 该算法被测试并应用于加速MLA DeepSeek-V3算子的推理，通过成功实现AIV与AIC计算的重叠，在单token处理场景下取得平均20%的性能增益。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过向量指令卸载窄矩阵乘法计算并重叠AIC/AIV计算，可有效提升昇腾NPU上的矩阵乘法性能，弥补了AscendC实用优化技术文献的空白。

Abstract: This research proposes and evaluates a novel approach to optimizing matrix multiplication (MatMul) on Huawei Ascend NPUs, motivated by a key insight: during matrix-vector multiplication (narrow MatMul), the Cube Unit (AIC) is often underutilized, while the Vector Unit (AIV) remains idle for most of the operator runtime. In this paper, we introduce the MatMul algorithm, which uses vector instructions of AscendC to effectively offload computations from the Cube Unit to the Vector Unit. The algorithm was tested and applied to accelerating the inference of MLA DeepSeek-V3 operator. By successfully overlapping AIV and AIC computations, our optimization showed a mean performance gain of 20% for a single token processing scenario. Our work addresses a significant gap in the literature on practical optimization techniques for AscendC, despite the availability of documentation and the active CANN community.

</details>


### [111] [BOA: Beamwidth Online Adaptation for Filtered-ANNS on a GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16175)
*Farhana Akter Tumpa,Rajiv Gupta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: BOA是一种单GPU过滤近似最近邻搜索引擎，通过在线波束宽度自适应，在多阶段搜索中按查询难度分配搜索开销，实现召回率与吞吐量的更好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的过滤ANNS系统为保证高召回，采用固定高波束宽度（100以上），即使批次中多数查询只需浅层搜索即可精确求解，也强制所有查询按最难查询的强度搜索，从而牺牲吞吐量。作者观察到，在多个数据集上，超过一半的查询用50或更小的波束宽度即可精确求解，因此需要按查询自适应分配搜索开销。

Method: 提出BOA（单GPU过滤ANNS引擎），核心是在多属性范围过滤下进行在线波束宽度自适应。采用多阶段搜索：先用窄波束评估所有查询，仅对结果不确定的查询用更宽波束逐步细化。由此召回率对初始波束宽度不敏感。BOA+进一步重叠各阶段执行以提升吞吐量。

Result: 对10,000个查询，在线自适应在平均波束宽度22至77时达到94.05%至99.96%的召回率；非自适应方法需固定波束宽度500才能达到相近或更低召回率。自适应使吞吐量提升7倍至12.5倍。

Conclusion: 在线波束宽度自适应能按查询难度定制搜索努力，在保持高召回的同时显著提高吞吐量，优于固定高波束宽度的现有方案。

Abstract: Filtered approximate nearest neighbor search, i.e. returning the top-k vectors nearest to a query vector among those satisfying one or more attribute predicates, has become a fundamental operation in modern vector search systems. Graph-based solutions employ beam search to solve a batch of queries in parallel for high throughput and employ high fixed beamwidth of 100 or greater for ensuring high recall. We observe that, given a batch of queries, more than half of the queries across multiple data sets can be solved precisely with a beamwidth of just 50 or less. Therefore, existing systems based on fixed high beamwidth sacrifice throughput to achieve high recall by forcing every query to search as thoroughly as the hardest query in the batch even though majority of queries can be resolved by a shallow search. In this paper we present a filtered ANNS engine for a single GPU named BOA that uses online beamwidth adaptation to customize the search effort across queries within a batch under multi-attribute range filters. We address the recall throughput tradeoff with a multi-phase search: all queries are first evaluated under a narrow beam, and only those with uncertain results are progressively refined with wider beamwidths. This renders recall largely insensitive to the starting beamwidth, whereas prior methods must use a fixed high beamwidth for high recall. BOA+ overlaps execution of phases to further enhance throughput. Our experiments show that, for 10,000 queries, online adaptation achieves 94.05% to 99.96% recall with average beamwidth ranging from 22 to 77, while a non-adaptive approach requires a fixed beamwidth of 500 to achieve similar or lower recall. Consequently, adaptivity increases throughput by 7x to 12.5x

</details>


### [112] [Cognitive Admission Control: Risk-Conditioned Assurance for Consequential Actions in Agentic Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16313)
*Jun He,Deying Yu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出认知准入控制（CAC），将"执行前需证据"显式化为策略约束，通过确定性评估器区分满足/违反/未决义务并触发针对性证据获取，成功准入生成绑定行动、见证清单与派发守卫的证书；原型在2,730次本地试验中验证无有害效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体分布式系统中，智能体可能被授权修改外部基础设施，却缺乏该修改已"准备就绪"的证据，这种证据缺失构成安全隐患。

Method: 将证据要求显式化：策略把带类型的行动及其建模风险映射到保证义务（谓词、证据类别、范围、时效性、见证集约束）；确定性评估器区分满足、违反与未决义务，对未决条件生成针对性证据获取请求；成功准入生成绑定行动、见证清单与派发时守卫的证书。形式化准入演算及其与中介执行的连接假设，并实现TypeScript原型。

Result: 在2,730次受控本地试验中（独立效果观测与匹配故障调度），390次CAC试验中120次效果无建模危害完成且无有害效果；实时策略基线完成数相同但接受了构造的相关见证失效。机制消融隔离出守卫、证据类别、结构切割与补救行为；额外9,000次测量覆盖完整本地派发路径与持久重放保护。

Conclusion: 保证是策略相对的：物理安全还需可靠证据、充分的环境模型以及效果执行过程中相关条件的保持。结果仅确立经测试的实现行为与本地成本，不代表生产环境故障率或语言模型能力比较。

Abstract: In agentic distributed systems, an agent may be authorized to mutate external infrastructure while lacking evidence that the mutation is ready to execute. Cognitive Admission Control (CAC) makes this evidence requirement explicit. A policy maps a typed action and its modeled risk to assurance obligations specifying predicates, evidence classes, scope, freshness, and witness-set constraints. A deterministic evaluator distinguishes satisfied, violated, and unresolved obligations; unresolved conditions produce targeted evidence-acquisition requests. Successful admission produces a certificate binding the action, its witness manifest, and dispatch-time guards.
  We formalize the admission calculus and the assumptions connecting it to mediated execution. The guarantees are policy-relative: physical safety additionally requires sound evidence, an adequate environment model, and preservation of relevant conditions through the effect. A TypeScript prototype is evaluated in 2,730 controlled local trials with independent effect observation and matched fault schedules. Across 390 CAC trials, 120 effects complete without modeled harm and no harmful effects occur. A live-policy baseline achieves the same completion count but admits the constructed correlated-witness failure. Mechanism ablations isolate guard, evidence-class, structural-cut, and remediation behavior. A further 9,000 measurements exercise the complete local dispatch path with persistent replay protection. These results establish tested implementation behaviors and local costs, not production failure rates or comparisons of language-model capability.

</details>


### [113] [PipeSwift: Revisiting Pipeline Parallelism for Large-Scale Completion-Oriented Agentic Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16491)
*Shiju Wang,Fei Ren,Fangcheng Fu,Zhanhong Tan,Kairui Li,Jingwei Cai,Kaisheng Ma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM智能体工作负载以完成时间为目标的特性，研究调度与并行策略，提出JCT感知的流水线并行运行时，显著降低任务完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM服务关注TTFT和TPOT等令牌级SLO，但智能体长时程工作负载更关注完成时间，现有服务设计不再适用，需重新审视调度与并行策略。

Method: 系统探索调度策略空间，发现JCT由prefill与decode效率的平衡决定，prefill优先调度虽TTFT最优但JCT次优，最优处于两个极端之间；发现流水线并行（PP）能提供有利的prefill-decode权衡。据此构建了开源的流水线并行运行时，采用JCT感知调度层与流水线集成多令牌预测的联合设计。

Result: 在64块H800 GPU上，使用两个360B+ MoE模型对真实编码和网络搜索智能体轨迹进行确定性回放，相比SGLang wide-EP降低JCT最高1.45倍，相比vLLM PP2降低2.33倍，相比当前最先进的开源PD分离部署降低1.54倍。

Conclusion: 面向完成时间的智能体工作负载需重新设计调度与并行策略，JCT感知的流水线并行运行时能有效平衡prefill与decode效率，显著降低整体任务完成时间。

Abstract: LLM agents execute long-horizon workflows where each model response determines the progress of subsequent tool interactions and environment transitions. Unlike chatbot serving, where TTFT and TPOT SLO constraints are critical, agentic workloads are increasingly governed by completion time. This shift challenges existing LLM serving designs, which are optimized around token-level SLOs.
  We revisit scheduling and parallelism under this completion-oriented objective. Through systematic exploration, we show that job completion time (JCT) is governed by the balance between prefill and decode efficiency. Prefill-prioritized scheduling, while achieving the best TTFT and decode throughput, renders suboptimal JCT; across the scheduling-policy space, completion time varies by up to 1.40$\times$, with the optimum at neither extreme. We further show that pipeline parallelism (PP), previously overlooked due to its limited decode latency advantage, benefits JCT by providing a favorable balance of prefill--decode trade-off.
  Based on these insights, we build \name{}, an optimized open-source pipeline-parallel runtime that co-designs scheduling and parallelism through a JCT-aware scheduling layer and pipeline-integrated multi-token prediction. Evaluated on deterministic replays of real coding and web-search agent trajectories with two 360B+ MoE models on 64 H800 GPUs, \name{} reduces overall JCT by up to 1.45$\times$ over SGLang wide-EP, 2.33$\times$ over vLLM PP2, and 1.54$\times$ over today's state-of-the-art open-source PD-disaggregated deployment.

</details>


### [114] [XMPIaaS: Towards Cloud Native MPI via Cooperative Process Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16531)
*Shunyu Yao,Dimitrios S. Nikolopoulos,Ali R. Butt*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出协作式MPI进程迁移系统，仅迁移受抢占影响的rank，避免全作业检查点开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HPC负载向云迁移后，面对临时资源、动态定价和可抢占实例，需在节点间迁移运行中的MPI进程而不重启作业。现有方法要么需从全局检查点重启整个作业，要么以极高复杂度透明拦截整个MPI栈。

Method: 设计协作式迁移系统：在MPI Sessions API之上暴露XMPI_quiesce接口，允许应用标记安全迁移点；扩展Hydra进程管理器编排完整迁移生命周期，包括rank静默、CRIU检查点/恢复、目标节点代理重启及rank无缝重连。

Result: 协作静默阶段占总迁移停机时间不足1.4%；停机时间仅由迁移节点的rank数量决定，与作业规模无关；插桩在正常运行中无额外开销。

Conclusion: 协作式选择性进程组迁移可在云环境中实现低成本、有弹性的MPI执行，避免全作业检查点代价。

Abstract: Message Passing Interface (MPI) has been the dominant programming model for High Performance Computing (HPC) for three decades, and as HPC workloads increasingly migrate to cloud infrastructure for scalability and cost efficiency, MPI applications must contend with an execution environment fundamentally unlike traditional supercomputers: ephemeral resources, dynamic pricing and preemptable instances. In such a volatile setting, the ability to relocate running MPI processes between nodes without restarting the job is a necessity for cost-effective, resilient execution. Existing approaches either require restarting the entire job from a global checkpoint, or transparently intercepting the full MPI stack at prohibitive complexity. To address these challenges, we propose \name, a cooperative migration system for MPI that enables selective process group migration on-the-fly. When a cloud instance is scheduled for preemption, only the affected ranks are relocated while the remaining processes briefly quiesce and resume in place, avoiding the cost of a full-job checkpoint. \name tackles this through a cooperative protocol between the MPI process management runtime and rank processes. We expose an \texttt{XMPI\_quiesce} interface built atop the MPI Sessions API that allows applications to mark safe migration points, and we extend the Hydra process manager to orchestrate the full migration lifecycle: rank quiescence, CRIU checkpoint/restore, proxy relaunch on the target node, and seamless rank reconnection. We evaluate and show that the cooperative quiesce phase accounts for less than 1.4\% of total migration downtime, and that this downtime is governed by the migrating node's rank count alone, independent of job size, and the instrumentation introduces no measurable overhead during normal execution.

</details>


### [115] [DeepShare: Assurance-Driven Deep Learning Job Scheduling for Multi-Tenant Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16682)
*Jinghao Wang,Yihang Zhou,Xiao Zhou,Xinlei Zheng,Xiaoyang Sun,Tianyu Wo,Chunming Hu,Renyu Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: DeepShare 用统一的租户保障信号在运行时协调配额借用、排队、抢占与 GPU 共享，在提升利用率的同时降低排队延迟并维持 QoS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多租户 GPU 集群常出现"利用率低但排队久"的矛盾现象，根源在于配额控制、队列排序、抢占和 GPU 共享各自依赖不同的局部信号，缺乏统一协调。

Method: 提出 DeepShare 调度器，以连续的租户保障（tenant-assurance）信号作为运行时控制回路，统一协调四项机制：弹性配额借用、租户级运行时预测、成本感知的尽力抢占、干扰感知的 MPS 共置；并用同一信号决定何时回收借出容量、何时收紧共享策略。

Result: 在 23,859 个 Venus 作业与 3,200 个内部作业的轨迹实验中，平均 GPU 利用率达 70.58%，较最强非侵入式共享基线提升 29.5%，平均排队延迟下降 46%；在 16-GPU Kubernetes 测试床上，平均作业完成时间减少 34%，保障型租户的 QoS 合规率达 93%。

Conclusion: 将租户保障视为运行时控制回路，比分别独立优化配额、调度与资源共享能取得更优的利用率—QoS 权衡。

Abstract: Multi-tenant GPU clusters frequently remain underutilized even when tenants experience long queueing delays, because quota control, queue ordering, preemption, and GPU sharing are driven by different local signals. We present DeepShare, a scheduler that uses a continuous tenant-assurance signal to coordinate these decisions at runtime. DeepShare combines elastic quota borrowing, tenant-specific runtime prediction, cost-aware best-effort preemption, and interference-aware MPS colocation, while using the same assurance signal to decide when borrowed capacity should be reclaimed and when sharing should become more conservative. In trace-driven experiments on 23,859 Venus jobs and 3,200 internal jobs, DeepShare achieves an average GPU utilization of 70.58%, a 29.5% improvement over the strongest non-intrusive sharing baseline, while reducing average queueing delay by 46%. On a 16-GPU Kubernetes testbed, it reduces the average job completion time by 34% and maintains 93% QoS compliance for guaranteed tenants. These results show that treating tenant assurance as a runtime control loop achieves a more advantageous utilization-QoS trade-off than optimizing quotas, scheduling, and resource sharing independently.

</details>


### [116] [The Price of Random Access: Measuring Block Granularity Across Four Compressed Formats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16731)
*Yakiv Shavidze*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文量化了压缩数据随机访问的密度代价：将254 MB档案切分为16 KiB独立寻址单元，绝对偏移格式仅付出1.632%代价，而可寻址zstd为6.57%；单元缩小到4 KiB时差距扩大（5.33% vs 10.06%）。低成本带来分裂免费、追加无需改格式、寻址延迟与位置无关、CPU/GPU共用同一档案等特性，并给出三个带证明与逐位验证的结构性结果及完整的测量工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机访问压缩数据通常要用密度来换取，但"汇率"从未被系统测量。作者希望在统一语料、多格式多维度下量化这一交换代价，并探讨低成本是否能让一系列通常不可得的性质成立。

Method: 在共同语料上跨四种格式、九个维度进行测量对比，比较绝对偏移格式与seekable zstd在不同单元大小（16 KiB、4 KiB）下的密度开销；对LZ77解析的重复距离链、自重叠匹配、依赖深度给出三个带证明的结构性结果并做逐位验证；所有结论标注等级（可复现命令、有理由的测量、估计），并单独发布测量工具（DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22713364，435条溯源记录）。

Result: 16 KiB单元下开销为1.632%（绝对偏移）vs 6.57%（seekable zstd）；4 KiB下为5.33% vs 10.06%，差距随单元缩小而扩大。附带特性：分裂档案免费且偶有收益（分块输入上-0.28%）、追加无需改格式、寻址延迟与位置无关、同一档案可被CPU和GPU解码器共用。另列出17个被否决方向及其数据，其中一项虽提升密度26%仍被拒。

Conclusion: 随机访问的密度代价远低于预期，使分裂、追加、常数寻址延迟和异构解码共用等性质成为可能；LZ77重复距离链构成可前缀扫描的替换幺半群、自重叠匹配具周期性、依赖深度存在编码器可强制的界，为低成本随机访问提供了结构性基础。

Abstract: Random access into compressed data is normally bought with density. We measure the exchange rate. Across four formats and nine axes on a common corpus, the cost of cutting a 254 MB archive into independently addressable 16 KiB units is 1.632% of the archive for an absolute-offset format against 6.57% for seekable zstd, and the gap widens as the unit shrinks: at 4 KiB, 5.33% against 10.06%. Because the cost is small, several properties follow that are usually unavailable: splitting an archive is free and occasionally profitable (-0.28% on tiled input), append needs no format change, seek latency does not depend on position, and one archive is read by both a CPU and a GPU decoder. We give three structural results with proofs and bit-perfect verification - that the repeat-distance chain of an LZ77 parse forms a substitution monoid and is therefore prefix-scannable without touching the bitstream, that self-overlapping matches are periodic rather than chained, and that dependency depth admits an encoder-enforced bound - and we report each measured limit together with the mechanism that sets it. Seventeen rejected directions are listed with their numbers, including one that improved density by 26% and was declined. Every claim carries a level: reproducible by command, measured with a stated reason, or estimated. The measurement tool is released separately (DOI 10.5281/zenodo.22713364) with 435 provenanced records.

</details>


### [117] [Nested Parallel von Neumann Architecture and Nested BSP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16787)
*Heng Liao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出两项相互嵌套的扩展：将 BSP 扩展为 Nested BSP（递归嵌套的并行计算范式），并将冯·诺依曼架构扩展为 Nested Parallel von Neumann Architecture（以统一总线为互连、端到端内存语义、层间对等），目标是让百万级处理器"仍是一台计算机"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 AI 计算不再是"单颗更强处理器"的竞赛，而是"一支处理器大军在一套指挥下仍像一台计算机"的问题。作者指出两大误区：一是把多台计算机连成网络就等于一台更大计算机的错误外推；二是几乎所有设计都默认"主从"范式（主机对设备、CPU 对加速器、中心对边缘）。因此需要从软件计算模型与硬件体系结构两个层面同时重新定义。

Method: 1) 将经典 BSP 递归嵌套，提出 Nested BSP：每一层内重复"并行推进—屏障—交换—聚合"四步，层内再嵌套层，规则是任意节点在任意层都是对等体（peer）。2) 将冯·诺依曼架构扩展为 Nested Parallel von Neumann Architecture：从封装到自治域层层嵌套，由统一总线（Unified Bus）端到端连接，具备完整内存语义与完全对等；形成"物理稀疏、逻辑紧密"的结构。3) 与华为 τ Scaling 定律配对：τ 治理每层如何折叠时间，架构治理嵌套并行计算机如何逐层、逐对等地"站立"。

Result: 论文在概念层面给出两个可互锁的扩展框架：Nested BSP 作为面向百万级并行的计算范式，Nested Parallel von Neumann Architecture 作为对应的体系结构，并以统一总线实现内存语义互连；两者共同支撑 τ Scaling 定律下的时间折叠机制，使多处理器在对等嵌套下表现为一台计算机。摘要未给出实验数据或量化性能结果。

Conclusion: 论文总结为两点：BSP 扩展为 Nested BSP，冯·诺依曼架构扩展为 Nested Parallel von Neumann Architecture。核心主张是"τ 折叠时间，对等并行逐层嵌套——众多处理器，仍是一台计算机"。

Abstract: Large-scale AI computing is no longer a contest of ''one stronger processor,'' but of how an army of processors under one command can still be one computer. This paper offers two interlocking extensions.
  First, extend BSP to Nested BSP. The Turing machine describes computation as a single tape, in sequence. A million processors need not a longer tape, but a battle plan nested layer within layer: at every layer, parallel work, barrier, exchange and aggregate, then the next phase. Every ``parallel advance'' inside a layer repeats the same four steps. Nested BSP extends classic BSP by nesting it recursively, a computing paradigm for million-scale parallelism, under one rule: every node at every layer is a peer.
  Second, extend von Neumann to the Nested Parallel von Neumann Architecture, and Unified Bus is its interconnect. Von Neumann taught us how to build one stored-program computer. The false extrapolation of eighty years was that wiring many computers into a network yields one larger computer. A second habit ran deeper: nearly every design assumes a master that commands and slaves that obey---host over device, CPU over accelerator, center over edge. The Nested Parallel Architecture extends that idea rather than discarding it. Two nesting dolls must fit: Nested BSP in software, and the Nested Parallel von Neumann Architecture from package to autonomous zone, joined by one memory-semantic bus end to end, with full peer equality: physically sparse, logically tight. It pairs with Huawei's $τ$ Scaling law: $τ$ governs how each layer folds time, while the Architecture governs how the nested parallel computer stands, layer by layer, peer by peer.
  In summary, the paper extends BSP to Nested BSP and extends von Neumann to the Nested Parallel von Neumann Architecture. $τ$ folds time, peer-equal parallelism nests layer by layer---many processors, still one computer.

</details>


### [118] [Byzantine Reliable Broadcast with Causal Ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17074)
*Mariarosaria Barbaraci,Christian Cachin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文研究拜占庭故障模型下的因果可靠广播，指出经典happened-before定义不足以防拜占庭进程篡改依赖信息，提出基于正确进程群组联合观测的因果消息排序新定义，并给出高效协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果有序广播多建立在全序广播之上，因果有序与可靠广播的结合很少；且仅有的方案采用经典的happened-before（基于单个进程事件）定义，该定义无法阻止拜占庭进程撒谎、遗漏、伪造依赖信息，从而破坏广播消息间的因果序，且单个观察者无法区分这种操纵与正确行为，论文通过前跑攻击展示了该问题。

Method: 扩展可靠广播概念以外化本地潜在知识，据此形式化拜占庭故障下可靠广播的第一个完整因果消息排序定义；该定义不同于经典定义，基于足够大一组正确进程的联合观测而非单个进程自身视角。在此基础上刻画保证因果序的拜占庭可靠广播信道的性质，并提出满足这些性质的协议。

Result: 所提协议可容忍最优的拜占庭故障数f < n/3；对于广播载荷消息m的一个实例，其比特复杂度为O(n^2(|m| + λ + n))，其中λ表示m的唯一（密码学）标签的最大长度。论文证明该协议实现了带因果排序的拜占庭可靠广播。

Conclusion: 论文揭示了经典因果定义在拜占庭环境下的缺陷及其导致的前跑攻击，提出基于正确进程联合观测的因果消息排序完整定义，并设计了容错最优、复杂度合理的协议，最终证明其正确性，填补了拜占庭可靠广播中因果有序研究的空白。

Abstract: Reliable and total-order broadcasts in the Byzantine-fault model are well studied, but adding causal order has received comparatively little attention, largely due to the complexity that stems from actions of Byzantine processes. Existing solutions almost exclusively build causal ordering on top of total-order broadcast. The combination of causal order with reliable broadcast remains rare, and the few solutions that exist adopt the classical definition of causality based on events occurring at individual processes (the happened-before relation). This definition is not sufficient to enforce causal ordering among broadcast messages: Byzantine processes can lie about, omit, and forge dependency information and thereby violate the causal order among self-reported events. Such manipulations remain indistinguishable from correct behavior to any single observer. We demonstrate the issue and its consequences via a front-running attack.
  To close this gap, we extend the notion of reliable broadcast to externalize local potential knowledge. We use this to formalize the first complete definition of causal message ordering in reliable broadcast under Byzantine faults. Unlike the classical formalization, this notion is grounded in the joint observations of a sufficiently large group of correct processes rather than a process's own view. Building on this definition, we characterize the properties of a Byzantine reliable broadcast channel that guarantees causal ordering. We then present an efficient protocol that satisfies these properties: it is resilient to the optimal number of $f < n/3$ Byzantine faults and for one instance that broadcasts payload message~$m$, it has bit complexity $O(n^2(|m| + λ+ n))$, where $λ$ denotes the maximal size of a unique (cryptographic) label for~$m$. Finally, we prove the protocol achieves Byzantine reliable broadcast with causal ordering.

</details>


### [119] [DS2-Based Cross-Data-Space Interoperability for Precision Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17185)
*Katerina Kyriakou,Ilias Syrigos,Ioannis Moutsinas,Panagiotis Tzimotoudis,Thanasis Korakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向互操作性的框架，支持不同农业数据空间参与者在安全、主权、透明的方式下共享数据产品与服务，构建跨数据空间（DigiAgro与AgroScience）的自动化生态，用于共享农场IoT测量、卫星影像、天气预报和分析服务，以生成如灌溉计划等作物实践建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代精准农业虽试图整合传统农业系统，但仍面临IoT数据碎片化、农民数据主权保护不足以及互操作性有限等挑战。论文旨在解决不同农业数据空间之间数据共享的这些障碍，在Horizon Europe DS2项目框架下推进跨部门数据交换。

Method: 采用分层参考架构，每一层由独立的操作模块构成，这些模块促进DigiAgro与AgroScience两个数据空间之间的跨部门数据交换，并以互操作性为导向支撑参与者的数据产品与服务共享。

Result: 实现了一个自动化生态系统，可跨不同数据空间共享多样化的农场IoT测量数据、卫星影像与指标、天气预报以及分析服务，进而生成准确的作物实践建议（如灌溉计划）。

Conclusion: 该框架为农民和农艺师提供可靠建议，有助于在维持可持续性的同时提高作物产量，展示了跨数据空间互操作与主权保护的可行性。

Abstract: Despite the strategies of modern precision agriculture to leverage the integration of legacy agricultural systems, the challenges of IoT data fragmentation, farmers' sovereignty preservation, and limited interoperability still persist. This paper presents our work, conducted within the Horizon Europe DS2 project (DataSpace, DataShare 2.0), that applies an interoperability-oriented framework supporting participants of different agricultural data spaces to share data products and services under secure, sovereign, and transparent methods. The suggested methodology follows a layered reference architecture, where each layer consists of independent operational modules that facilitate the inter-sector data exchange between DigiAgro and AgroScience Data Spaces. The result of this work is an automated ecosystem for sharing diverse farm IoT measurements, satellite images and metrics, weather forecasts, and analytics services across distinct data spaces, aiming to generate accurate recommendations on crop practices, such as irrigation schedules, that farmers and agronomists will rely on to increase crop production while maintaining sustainability.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [How Can We Shrink the Family of Test Databases? Query Containment with Nulls and Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16218)
*Helen Sternbach,Sara Cohen*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究在含空值的数据库（SQL三值语义）和含顺序比较的连接查询中，如何缩减判定查询包含所需测试数据库族的规模，并给出固定参数可处理性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统连接查询的包含与等价判定只需在一个正则数据库上评估即可，但这一经典方法在两类真实场景下失效：含空值的数据库（三值语义）和含顺序比较的查询。在这两种情形下，判定包含是Π₂^p完全的，既有刻画需要指数级测试数据库族，缺乏实用的等价性认证途径。

Method: 针对含空值数据库上的连接查询，将测试数据库族缩小到仅对一组特殊变量呈指数规模，当该集合大小为常数时把包含判定放入NP。针对含比较的查询，构造能判定包含的正则值，并通过将测试分解为独立组件、沿顺序冲突拆分来缩小测试族。最后将两类特征结合，证明测试数据库数量关于两个查询的三个局部参数是固定参数可处理的。

Result: 得到了缩减后的测试数据库族：空值情形下指数仅依赖于特殊变量集合；比较情形下通过正则值与分解拆分缩减测试；组合情形下测试数据库数量关于三个局部参数固定参数可处理，且空值-only与比较-only测试为其特例。

Conclusion: 每种测试只需在数据库系统原生的操作上，将包含查询在一族数据库上求值即可实现，为等价性认证提供了更实用的路径。

Abstract: Query containment and equivalence drive database query optimization and rewriting. For plain conjunctive queries, both are decided by evaluating one query over a single canonical database of the other. This classical test breaks down in two settings that pervade real queries: databases with null values under SQL's three-valued semantics, and queries with order comparisons. In both, deciding containment is $Π_2^p$-complete, and the known characterizations replace it by an exponential family of test databases, leaving no practical route to certifying equivalence.
  We ask how the family of test databases can be shrunk. For conjunctive queries over databases with nulls, we shrink the family to one that is exponential only in a special set of variables, and place containment in NP when that set has constant size. For queries with comparisons, we construct canonical values that decide containment, and then shrink the family by decomposing the test into independent components and by splitting it along the order conflicts. Finally, we combine the two features, and prove that the number of test databases is fixed-parameter tractable in three local parameters of the two queries, with the null-only and comparison-only tests as special cases. Each test evaluates the containing query over a family of databases, an operation native to any database system.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Coaching Qwen3 Coder 30B to Think Like a CodeClash Arena Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16096)
*Ivy Ning Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 针对弱代码智能体在长程竞技交互中的失败问题，提出 ReAct SFT 与轨迹质量加权 SFT，微调后的 Qwen3-Coder-30B 在 CodeClash 锦标赛中超越了原版 Qwen3 Coder Plus。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型代码智能体虽已实用，但弱模型或开放权重模型仍难以可靠理解用户意图并执行多步工作流，尤其在需反复检查历史结果、诊断失败、在交互约束下选择下一步编辑的长程场景中。研究动机在于探索如何借助强智能体的蒸馏知识提升弱代码智能体的"思考过程"。

Method: 在 CodeClash（多轮锦标赛式代码竞技基准，原工作评估 8 个商业代码智能体、6 个竞技场）中，以排名最末的 Qwen3 Coder Plus 对应的开放权重模型 Qwen3-Coder-30B 为案例。分析发现其存在语法与协议违规错误、跨轮战略适应能力弱等问题，且离线 SFT 无法直接验证生成动作的有效性与收益。据此提出两种方法：ReAct SFT，将教师轨迹改写为显式的 [obs][thought][act] 链；轨迹质量加权 SFT，对样本重新加权以鼓励编辑后检查行为。

Result: ReAct SFT 显著改善了战略行为，微调后的模型在锦标赛评估中性能超过原始 Qwen3 Coder Plus。

Conclusion: 将强智能体的推理结构（显式观察-思考-行动链）与轨迹质量加权相结合，可有效弥补弱代码智能体在竞技式长程交互中的短板，为提升开放权重代码智能体提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large language model coding agents have recently become useful for software tasks, but weaker or open-weight agents still struggle to reliably interpret user intent and execute complex multi-step workflows. This gap is especially visible in long-horizon settings, where an agent must repeatedly inspect prior outcomes, diagnose failure, and choose the next code edit under interaction constraints. It motivates a natural question: what can we do to improve the thinking process of a weak code agent? We study this question in CodeClash, a code-arena benchmark where the original work evaluates 8 commercial coding agents across 6 arenas through multi-round tournaments. Since Qwen3 Coder Plus ranks last among them, we take the open-weight Qwen3-Coder-30B as a case study and investigate how to improve it with distilled knowledge from stronger agents. Our analysis shows that Qwen3-Coder-30B is not well optimized for arena-style interaction: it frequently produces syntax and protocol-breaking errors and exhibits weak strategic adaptation across rounds. These failures are difficult to correct with vanilla instruction tuning alone, since offline SFT cannot directly verify whether a generated action is valid or beneficial. To address this, we propose ReAct SFT, which rewrites teacher trajectories into explicit [obs][thought][act] chains, and trajectoryquality weighted SFT, which reweights samples to encourage post-edit checking. ReAct SFT substantially improves strategic behavior, and our fine-tuned model outperforms the original Qwen3 Coder Plus in tournament evaluation.

</details>


### [122] [Docker Containers vs. Virtual Machines: A Comparative Study of Architecture, Performance, Configuration, and Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16148)
*Faraz Gurramkonda,Akanksha Malla,Sayma Tamboli,Shayesta Nazneen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于文献对虚拟机和Docker容器进行了多维度比较分析，认为两者在效率与隔离性之间存在设计权衡，混合架构（容器运行于加固虚拟机内）是云与多租户场景中的实用折中方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代应用平台需在隔离工作负载的同时兼顾部署速度、可移植性、资源效率与安全性；虚拟机与容器在不同抽象层实现该需求，但学界与业界对二者的优劣缺乏统一结论，因此有必要进行系统性的比较分析。

Method: 采用基于文献的对比分析方法，从架构、配置与生命周期管理、性能、可扩展性和安全性等维度，对虚拟机和Docker容器进行系统梳理与比较。

Result: 既有研究普遍显示，容器具有更短启动时间、更小镜像、更高工作负载密度以及接近原生的执行性能，但这些优势受工作负载特征、存储与网络驱动、资源控制及实验设计影响；虚拟机的开销更大，但提供独立内核、异构客户操作系统和更强的隔离边界。

Conclusion: 效率与隔离性应被视为设计权衡，而非判定某一技术普遍更优；在云与多租户系统中，将容器运行于加固虚拟机内的混合架构通常能提供较为实用的平衡。

Abstract: Modern application platforms must isolate workloads while preserving deployment speed, portability, resource efficiency, and security. Virtual machines (VMs) and Docker containers address this requirement at different abstraction layers: VMs virtualize hardware and run independent guest operating systems, whereas containers isolate processes while sharing the host kernel. This paper presents a comparative, literature-based analysis of the two approaches across architecture, configuration and lifecycle management, performance, scalability, and security. Published studies generally associate containers with shorter startup times, smaller images, higher workload density, and near-native execution for many workloads. These benefits depend on workload characteristics, storage and network drivers, resource controls, and experimental design. VMs introduce greater overhead but offer independent kernels, heterogeneous guest operating systems, and a stronger isolation boundary. The comparison therefore treats efficiency and isolation as a design trade-off rather than declaring one technology universally superior. A hybrid architecture, in which containers run inside hardened VMs, often provides a practical balance for cloud and multi-tenant systems.

</details>


### [123] [Models as Governed Interfaces for AI-Native MBSE: Read-Side Adequacy and Write-Side Admissibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16252)
*Jason Gower,Michael J. de C. Henshaw,Siyuan Ji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文指出仅让SysML v2等模型可被程序访问不足以保证AI可靠参与系统工程，提出"认知充分性"数据架构属性（读侧四条准则+参与侧一条假设），以Apollo 11公开SysML v2重建模型为案例，并提出受治理查询架构框架（GQAF），且设定可证伪的评测方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作把"机器可读/可程序访问"当作AI参与系统工程的充分条件，但作者认为这只是必要条件。真正的瓶颈不在建模语言，而在其周边的数据架构：当AI查询结构完整的模型以求推导时，会遇到推导链缺失、认知状态未标注、来源信息缺失、证据无法在模型内解析等问题；面对这些缺口，AI不会弃权，而会用训练数据填补，而该来源既不可验证也不受治理。

Method: 选取按当前实践可称"典范"而非有缺陷的公开Apollo 11 SysML v2重建模型作为案例进行探查。将所缺失的属性命名为"认知充分性"，作为候选数据架构模式，分为两半：读侧充分性（推导、状态与来源应能回答查询而非诱发猜测）与写侧可采纳性（在AI贡献进入记录前加以把关）。该属性拆解为五条准则：四条位于读侧，由案例与收敛文献佐证；第五条位于参与侧，作为本文尚未检验的假设提出。架构空间从内联元数据扩展延伸到基底原生的多模型存储，并据此提出"受治理查询架构框架"（GQAF），通过工程师既有的视点约定来治理智能体参与。

Result: 四条读侧准则得到案例证据与收敛文献的支持；第五条参与侧准则为待检验假设。作者将这一重构交由证伪：若认知层在同一模型上无法击败检索增强基线，则视为被反驳。

Conclusion: 仅有机器可读性不足以支撑可信的AI参与；需要以认知充分性为核心的数据架构（GQAF）。该框架先于Apollo推导链上检验，随后进入工业试点，并以可证伪方式界定其有效性。

Abstract: Machine-readable models such as SysML v2 are now programmatically accessible, and a growing body of work treats that access as the enabling condition for AI participation in systems engineering. Access is necessary, but not sufficient. The remaining work lies not in the modelling language but in the data architecture around it. An AI reader that queries a structurally complete model for a derivation still runs into absent derivation chains, untagged epistemic status, missing provenance, and evidence that the model cannot resolve. Faced with these gaps, it does not abstain; it fills them from training data, a source that is neither verifiable nor governed. To make the case on a model that is exemplary by current practice rather than deficient, we probe the public Apollo 11 SysML v2 reconstruction. We name the missing property epistemic adequacy and offer it as a candidate data-architecture pattern in two halves. Read-side adequacy lets derivation, status, and provenance answer a query rather than invite a guess; write-side admissibility gates an AI contribution before it enters the record. The property is broken down into five criteria. Four sit on the read side, evidenced by the case and convergent literature; the fifth sits on the participation side, advanced as a hypothesis this paper does not yet test. The architecture space runs from an inline metadata extension up to a substrate-native multi-model store, and over it, we propose the Governed-Query Architecture Framework, which governs agent participation through the viewpoint conventions that engineers already use. We commit the reframing to falsification: the epistemic layer counts as refuted if it cannot beat a retrieval-augmented baseline on the same model, tested first on the Apollo chain and then in an industrial pilot.

</details>


### [124] [AgentGuard: Learning Execution Guardrails from Anomalous Coding-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16287)
*Wuyang Dai,Song Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgentGuard:从历史失败轨迹中学习指令级行为约束,提升AI编程智能体执行的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程智能体依赖执行框架与代码仓库和外部工具交互,但任务成功并不等于执行可靠。智能体可能修改无关文件、重写测试、执行不安全命令或忽略失败的验证,因此需要行为护栏来保障执行的可靠性。

Method: 提出AgentGuard——一种指令级护栏框架,从编码智能体的异常轨迹中学习条件性执行约束。方法不依赖人工指定安全规则,而是自动提取反复出现的执行失败模式,将其泛化为指令级行为约束,并组织为一个轻量级护栏技能(skill),仅在当前指令相关时动态激活对应规则。

Result: 基于642条真实编程智能体执行中记录的失败轨迹(覆盖382个仓库任务)进行评估。护栏从461条轨迹(282个任务)中学习,在不相交的100个任务上测试。以Claude Code搭配Claude Haiku 4.5为底层智能体,相比基线,异常执行率从69.0%降至26.7%,成功任务完成率从21.7%提升至35.0%。

Conclusion: 从历史失败中学习到的执行护栏能显著提升AI编程智能体的可靠性,同时也凸显出在安全性与任务完成率之间取得平衡仍是待解决的挑战。

Abstract: AI coding agents increasingly rely on execution harnesses to interact with repositories and external tools. However, task success does not guarantee reliable execution. Agents may still modify unrelated files, rewrite tests, issue unsafe commands, or ignore failed validations, motivating behavioral guardrails for reliable execution. We present AgentGuard, an instruction-level guardrail framework that learns conditional execution constraints from anomalous trajectories of coding agents. Rather than relying on manually specified safety rules, AgentGuard automatically extracts recurring execution failure patterns, generalizes them into instruction-level behavioral constraints, and organizes them as a lightweight guardrail skill that dynamically activates only the rules relevant to the current instruction.
  This design enables behavioral guidance while minimizing unnecessary restrictions on normal execution. We evaluate AgentGuard using 642 documented failure traces collected from real coding-agent executions across 382 repository tasks. Guardrails are learned from 461 traces covering 282 tasks and evaluated on a disjoint set of 100 tasks. Using Claude Code with Claude Haiku 4.5 as the underlying coding agent, we compare the baseline agent with the same agent augmented by AgentGuard. Experimental results show that AgentGuard reduces the Abnormal Execution Rate from 69.0% to 26.7% and increases the Successful Task Completion Rate from 21.7% to 35.0%. These results demonstrate that execution guardrails learned from historical failures can substantially improve the reliability of AI coding agents while highlighting the remaining challenge of balancing safety and task completion.

</details>


### [125] [Assurance Envelopes for Autonomous Coding Agents: Minimum-Cost Evidence for Software Change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16302)
*Anjan Goswami*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"任务条件化保障包"(task-conditioned assurance envelope)概念：给定变更需保持的性质(义务)，从可用证据(测试、类型检查、证明、静态分析、轨迹)中选择最小代价子集，使前向链式推理能重新建立这些义务。证据与组合规则构成类型化推理图，选择结果通过闭包验证。实验表明最小保障包依赖于任务，部分性质需多个证据协同，且难度由结构而非规模决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体回到已有软件时会继承先前工程产生的证据，但重新加载全部证据浪费资源，而丢弃变更所依赖的证据又会导致必需性质失去支撑。因此需要确定：给定变更必须保持的性质(义务)，哪一最小代价的证据子集能够重新建立它们。

Method: 将证据及组合规则建模为类型化推理图；义务在从所选证据前向链式推理到达时即被满足；通过该闭包验证每个选择，而非信任优化器。软件衍生图来自先前AI编码智能体运行的保留产物并被冻结，进而求解应为后续任务恢复哪些累积证据。采用CP-SAT优化求解，并设计249个实例的预设合成基准。

Result: 小规模图(Rust、IronBlocks、Pong)显示最小保障包依赖任务；当现有证据无法重建所需性质时可能不存在保障包；某些性质需多个证据共同支撑；扩展需求会增加而非替换证据。合成基准中，丢弃"多证据协同"结构的基线必然无法重导出这些性质；所有完成的精确交叉校验均与CP-SAT优化器一致；500证据图的中位求解时间低于20 ms，但每个目标含大量替代推导的图在更小规模时即超时，说明难度由结构而非原始规模决定。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是将已有优化方法有界地应用于为软件变更选择保障上下文；发现义务及下游智能体收益仍是开放问题。

Abstract: When a coding agent returns to existing software, it inherits evidence from earlier engineering work: tests, type checks, proofs, static analyses, and traces. Reloading all of it is wasteful, but dropping a piece the change depends on can leave a required property unsupported. Given the properties a change must preserve, its obligations, we ask which least-cost subset of the available evidence re-establishes them, and we call such a subset a task-conditioned assurance envelope. Evidence and the rules that combine it form a typed inference graph; an obligation is met when forward chaining from the selected evidence reaches it, and we validate every selection by that closure rather than by trusting the optimizer. The software-derived graphs in our evaluation come from preserved outcomes of prior AI coding-agent runs; we freeze those artifacts and ask which accumulated evidence should be restored for a later task. Small graphs from Rust, IronBlocks, and Pong outcomes show that the minimum envelope depends on the task, that none may exist when current evidence cannot re-establish a required property, that some properties need several pieces of evidence together, and that expanding the requirements adds evidence rather than replacing it. A prespecified synthetic benchmark of 249 instances characterizes computation: a baseline that discards the 'several pieces together' structure necessarily fails to re-derive them; every completed exact cross-check agreed with the CP-SAT optimizer; and median solve time stayed below 20 ms at 500-evidence graphs, except that graphs with many alternative derivations per target timed out at far smaller sizes, so structure, not raw size, drives difficulty. The contribution is a bounded application of established optimization to selecting assurance context for a software change; discovering the obligations and downstream agent benefit remain open.

</details>


### [126] [FairLint-DL: An IDE-Native Tool for Fairness Debugging of Deep Learning Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16321)
*Archit Rathod,Saeid Tizpaz-Niari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FairLint-DL 是一个 VS Code 扩展，将公平性检测左移到预训练阶段的 IDE 内，在表格数据上直接进行偏差检测，并以信息论 QID 指标量化受保护属性对预测的因果影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性分析工具多为训练后评估框架，需在完成整个模型开发流程后才能评估偏差，检测滞后、成本高，难以融入开发者日常工作流。

Method: 训练可配置深度神经网络作为代理模型；采用基于香农熵与最小熵的信息论 QID 指标；实现两阶段梯度引导搜索以发现歧视实例；通过敏感性分析将偏差定位到特定网络层与神经元的因果调试流水线；并集成 SHAP 与 LIME 双可解释性引擎进行特征级归因。

Result: 在 Adult Census Income、German Credit 和 Bank Marketing 三个表格基准上评估，公平性问题因数据集差异很大：Adult 上 96.0% 的实例 QID 超过 0.1 bit 显著性阈值，平均 QID 为 0.619 bit，差别影响比为 0.581，违反五分之四法则；缓存模型下 12 秒内即可产出结果。

Conclusion: FairLint-DL 证明了将公平性分析集成进开发者工作流且不带来显著开销的可行性，实现了预训练阶段的 IDE 原生偏差检测。

Abstract: Existing fairness analysis tools predominantly operate as post-training evaluation frameworks, requiring practitioners to complete the full model development lifecycle before assessing bias. We present FairLint-DL, a Visual Studio Code extension that implements a shift-left approach to fairness testing by enabling pre-training, IDE-native bias detection directly on tabular datasets. FairLint-DL trains a configurable deep neural network as a proxy model and applies information-theoretic Quantitative Individual Discrimination (QID) metrics. Grounded in Shannon and min-entropy, QID quantifies the causal influence of protected attributes on predictions. The system implements a two-phase gradient-guided search algorithm for discovering discriminatory instances, a causal debugging pipeline that localizes bias to specific network layers and neurons via sensitivity analysis, and dual explainability engines using SHAP and LIME for feature-level attribution. Evaluation on three tabular benchmarks (Adult Census Income, German Credit, and Bank Marketing) reveals fairness concerns that vary widely across datasets: on Adult, 96.0% of analyzed instances exhibit QID above the 0.1-bit significance threshold, with a mean QID of 0.619 bits and a disparate impact ratio of 0.581, violating the four-fifths legal rule. FairLint-DL produces these results within 12 seconds on cached models, demonstrating the feasibility of integrating fairness analysis into the developer workflow without significant overhead.

</details>


### [127] [Protocol-Preserving Context Trimming for Agentic Workflows: Benefits, Failure Regimes, and Budget Guardrails](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16461)
*Harish Gaggar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文评估了"协议保持式上下文裁剪"作为多步智能体工作流中受可靠性约束的方法，发现自适应预算护栏在保留56% token节省的同时，可将任务成功率提升至96.0%、协议遵循度达96.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体LLM系统依赖长交互历史来保存指令、工具状态、中间决策和未解决依赖，但无限制的上下文增长会提高计算成本并降低效率。因此需要在保证可靠性的前提下缩减上下文。

Method: 比较五种裁剪策略（基于新近度、基于相关性、摘要、协议感知裁剪、自适应预算护栏），在不同保留上下文比例和工作流复杂度类别下进行评测，指标包括任务成功率、协议遵循度、有效工具调用、token节省、延迟降低、级联失败和关键上下文阈值。

Result: 常规策略平均节省约60% token，但任务成功率较低（66.6–77.3%）、协议遵循度85.5–88.6%；协议感知裁剪将任务成功率提升至92.2%；自适应护栏实现96.0%任务成功率、96.3%协议遵循度、1.0%级联失败率，平均节省56.0% token。保留上下文预算≤25%时失败几率是≥50%的10.92倍（p<0.001）；激进预算下协议感知裁剪比常规方法成功几率高5.24倍，自适应护栏较固定协议感知裁剪再提升2.11倍（p<0.001）；关键上下文阈值随工作流复杂度上升。

Conclusion: 可靠的上下文缩减更多取决于保留协议关键状态，而非最大化token删除量；自适应护栏可提升长时程智能体系统的效率、可扩展性与可靠性。

Abstract: Agentic large language model (LLM) systems rely on long interaction histories to preserve instructions, tool states, intermediate decisions, and unresolved dependencies, but unrestricted context growth increases computational cost and can reduce efficiency. This study evaluates protocol-preserving context trimming as a reliability-constrained approach for multi-step agentic workflows. Five trimming strategies - recency-based, relevance-based, summarization, protocol-aware trimming, and adaptive budget guardrails - were compared across retained-context levels and workflow-complexity classes using task success, protocol adherence, valid tool calls, token savings, latency reduction, cascading failures, and critical context thresholds. Conventional strategies achieved about 60% mean token savings but lower task success (66.6-77.3%) and protocol adherence (85.5-88.6%). Protocol-aware trimming improved task success to 92.2%, while adaptive guardrails achieved 96.0% task success, 96.3% protocol adherence, and 1.0% cascading failure with 56.0% mean token savings. Retained-context budgets of 25% or less increased failure odds 10.92-fold relative to budgets of 50% or more (p < 0.001). Protocol-aware trimming produced 5.24-fold greater odds of successful completion than conventional methods under aggressive budgets, while adaptive guardrails further increased success odds 2.11-fold versus fixed protocol-aware trimming (p < 0.001). Critical context thresholds also increased with workflow complexity. These findings indicate that reliable context reduction depends more on preserving protocol-critical state than on maximizing token removal, and that adaptive guardrails can improve efficiency, scalability, and reliability in long-horizon agentic systems.

</details>


### [128] [AI Policies: Help or Hindrance? A Software Developer's Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16496)
*Samuel Ferino,Rashina Hoda,John Grundy,Christoph Treude,Hashini Gunatilake*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对19名软件开发者的访谈,研究揭示了AI政策对开发者的帮助与阻碍,并提出以开发者为中心的政策制定方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件组织为降低LLM相关风险(如敏感信息泄露、未授权使用)而制定的AI政策,若开发者不参与、不采纳则形同虚设。因此需要理解开发者与AI政策互动的真实情况。

Method: 采用定性研究方法,对19名软件开发者进行访谈,分析AI政策在实践中如何帮助和阻碍开发者。

Result: 识别出AI政策对开发者的双重作用:既有支持性的一面,也存在阻碍性的一面。

Conclusion: 为管理者和决策者提出以开发者为中心的方式引入AI政策的建议,以提高政策的有效性与采纳度。

Abstract: AI policies introduced by software organisations to mitigate LLM-related risks such as sensitive information leaks and unauthorised usage are not useful if software developers do not engage with them. We draw on 19 software developer interviews to show how AI policies help and hinder developers. We suggest approaches to support managers and decision makers with a developer-centric approach to introducing AI policies.

</details>


### [129] [ExecuCritic: Calibrated Critic Shaping for Code Generation with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16604)
*Junjie Cao,Yingjie He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出ExecuCritic联合训练框架，用可执行反馈训练critic与coder，改善信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行反馈（单元测试）虽客观直接，但常被压缩为单一通过/失败位，导致RLVR面临困难的信用分配问题；现有代码系统中的reviewer/tester角色多为提示而非训练，且未与执行结果校准。

Method: ExecuCritic在同一批执行rollout上联合更新coder与critic。critic预测通过/失败结果并给出简短诊断反馈；仅当critic与执行器在当前rollout组上一致时，coder才使用该信号。

Result: 在八个代码基准与两个近期开源骨干模型上，ExecuCritic优于无critic的GRPO、提示式reviewer系统及标量奖励模型基线，同时所需策略梯度步数与沙箱执行次数更少。

Conclusion: 消融与可靠性分析表明，性能提升源于更好的信用分配，而非更大的采样预算。

Abstract: Execution feedback is a useful supervision signal for code models because unit tests are objective and directly measure program correctness. Its weakness is that an entire program is often reduced to one pass or fail bit, leaving RLVR to solve a difficult credit assignment problem. At the same time, coding systems often include separate reviewer or tester roles, but these critics are usually prompted rather than trained and are not calibrated against execution. We propose ExecuCritic, a joint training framework in which a coder and a critic are updated on the same execution rollouts. The critic predicts pass or fail outcomes and gives short diagnostic feedback; the coder uses this signal only when the critic agrees with the executor on the current rollout group. Across eight code benchmarks and two recent open backbones, ExecuCritic improves over GRPO without a critic, prompted reviewer systems and scalar reward model baselines, while requiring fewer policy gradient steps and fewer sandbox executions. Ablations and reliability analyses suggest that the gains come from better credit assignment rather than larger sampling budgets.

</details>


### [130] [An Exploratory Study of Dependabot Cooldown Adoption in Open-Source GitHub Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16605)
*Hidetake Tanaka,Rikuto Tsuchida,Kazumasa Shimari,Raula Gaikovina Kula,Kenichi Matsumoto*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对GitHub Dependabot冷却期功能的早期采用情况进行了探索性实证研究，分析流行开源仓库如何采用、配置该功能及背后的动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化依赖更新可能在维护者和社区察觉前迅速传播恶意软件包，2025年7月GitHub正式推出Dependabot冷却期以防御软件供应链攻击，但其早期采用效果尚不明确。

Method: 对92个已知采用动机的事件、75个仅出于安全考虑的采用事件，以及保留冷却期的仓库中251个生态系统进行实证分析，考察采用动机与配置方式。

Result: 92个已知动机的采用事件中有83个源于安全考虑；75个仅安全采用中有43个由安全linter警告触发。251个生态系统中97.2%设置了通用延迟，其中64.3%使用七天，而各更新类型设置的采用率均低于10%。

Conclusion: 早期采用者偏好简单的默认延迟而非细粒度控制，建议工具提供反映生态支持的稳健默认值，并将细粒度控制保留给更新优先级明确的依赖项。

Abstract: Automated dependency updates can rapidly propagate malicious package releases before maintainers and the broader community have enough time to detect them. In July 2025, GitHub made Dependabot cooldown generally available as a defense against software supply chain attacks. However, the effects of its early adoption remain unknown. In this exploratory study, we empirically examine how popular open-source GitHub repositories adopt and configure the feature and investigate their motivations. We find that security concerns motivated 83 of 92 adoption events with known motivations. Security linter warnings triggered 43 of 75 security-only adoptions. Among 251 ecosystems within repositories that retained cooldown, 97.2% set a general delay. Of these, 64.3% used seven days, while use of each update type setting was below 10%. Early adopters therefore favor simple default delays over fine-grained controls. These findings suggest that tools could provide robust defaults reflecting ecosystem support and reserve fine-grained controls for dependencies with clear update priorities.

</details>


### [131] [Memory-Skill Isomorphism: One Skill Carrier, Two Native Uses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16669)
*Kang Ruiyuan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将记忆与技能统一到同一"渐进式披露"载体(Skill)上，用受治理的写入路径区分历史与当前状态；实测显示治理是主要瓶颈，L1经验点以极小成本换取失败率下降，但结论属选定任务上的存在性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆与技能均在提升智能体能力时沿用一套重复的复用机制（存储、路由、检索、反思、更新路径），随积累而膨胀。作者认为这种重复不必全部重建：Skill 本身即是蒸馏记忆的天然载体，因此希望用单一载体同时承载记忆与能力，并厘清"写入治理"这一被忽视的环节。

Method: 提出以 Skill 作为记忆的统一载体，按三级渐进披露组织：L0 常驻描述（受治理的 1,024 字符预算，承载热线索）、L1 按需 SKILL.md（冷索引与策展策略）、L2 reference/*.md（详细记忆）。记忆与能力共享读取侧，反思可演进任一 Skill；写入侧则分叉——历史追加、当前状态重写并重新校验。在已部署系统中对这一实现（常驻索引）进行评测：统计 4 条写入条目中到达结算账本的路径数，测量 L1 经验点的令牌开销与基线对比（k=1、k=5、会话总计），并在一个选定任务、单一模型上比较暴露经验前后的失败数。

Result: 治理是最锐利的识别点：共有 4 条写入条目，仅 1/4 到达结算账本。定价工作点上，一个 L1 lesson-1 点增加 1,313 个首轮令牌，基线为 1,462；k=1 时二者近似持平（1,313 对 1,365），k=5 时为 4,949；会话总量仅相差 1.18 倍且末轮区间重叠，价格分解不可得。在单个选定任务、单一模型上，暴露该 lesson 后失败减少（0/8 或 1/8，对比非并发的历史地板 6/6，未做多重性校正）。常驻索引与 BM25 在两个小规模对比中未检测到差异。

Conclusion: 结果支持一条候选 RSI 设计规则：单一 Skill 载体族、共享读取侧、受治理的差异化写入。但需注意：任务系基于先前地板证据选定，故证据属选定任务上的事后存在性信号，而非确证性速率；身体传递 h := P(D|A) 在 RQ1–RQ2 中未被仪器化，本文评估的只是常驻索引实现。

Abstract: Memory and skills improve agents without changing weights: memory carries prior experience, skills carry reusable procedures. Wrapping both in stores, routers, retrieval, reflection, and update paths makes reuse machinery grow with accumulation. Part of this duplication need not be rebuilt: a Skill is already a natural carrier for distilled memory. Here the memory component is a Skill: resident description holds hot cues, on-demand SKILL.md a colder index and curation policy, and reference/*.md files detailed memories (levels L0 -> L1 -> L2).
