A governed 1,024-character description budget keeps the resident index compressed. Memory and capability thus share one progressive-disclosure carrier; reflection evolves either Skill. Writes fork: history appended, current state rewritten and revalidated. In one deployed system, the sharpest identification is governance: 4 write entries exist, but only 1/4 reaches the settlement ledger. At the priced operating point, one L1 lesson-1 point adds 1,313 first-turn tokens against a 1,462-token baseline--tied at k=1 (1,313 versus 1,365), 4,949 at k=5; session totals differ by only 1.18x with overlapping last-turn ranges; price decomposition is unavailable. On one selected task with one model, exposing the lesson means fewer failures (0/8 or 1/8 with the lesson versus a shared non-concurrent 6/6 historical floor, unadjusted for multiplicity). The task was selected on prior floor evidence, so this is a selected-task post-selection existence signal, not a confirmatory rate. Resident and BM25 show no detected difference in two small comparisons. This motivates a candidate RSI design rule: one Skill carrier family, a shared read side, governed distinct writes. Body delivery h := P(D|A) is uninstrumented in RQ1--RQ2; this evaluates a resident-index implementation.

</details>


### [132] [RECTIFY: An Interactive Workbench for Post-Evaluation RAG Diagnosis, Repair, and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16764)
*Keerthana Murugaraj,Salima Lamsiyah,Martin Theobald*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RECTIFY 是一个交互式 Streamlit 工作台，将 RAG 评测案例转化为可审计的修复工作流，帮助开发者决定下一步修复什么。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 评测器虽能识别弱检索、接地不良、答案不完整、生成无依据等失败，但很少帮助开发者决定接下来该修复什么，缺少从评测结果到修复决策的桥梁。

Method: RECTIFY 先过滤不需要修复的案例，将剩余失败路由为可操作的失败族与细粒度修复切片，并生成可编辑的修复卡片，开发者可批准、拒绝或通过沙箱重跑进行验证。

Result: 在受控 RAG 基准上，RECTIFY 呈现了可解释的失败画像：BM25 主要触发噪声检索类修复，而稠密与混合检索留下较少的多部分欠检索和证据未充分利用案例。预过滤减少了不必要的修复候选，切片级路由比宽泛的族级诊断产生更有针对性的修复卡片。

Conclusion: RECTIFY 作为开源 Streamlit 工作台公开可用，帮助开发者将评测结果转化为可检查的修复决策。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) evaluators can identify failures such as weak retrieval, poor grounding, incomplete answers, and unsupported generation, but they rarely help developers decide what to repair next. We present RECTIFY, an interactive Streamlit workbench that turns evaluated RAG cases into auditable repair workflows. RECTIFY filters cases that do not require repair, routes remaining failures into actionable families and finegrained repair slices, and generates editable repair cards that developers can approve, reject, or verify through sandbox reruns. On a controlled RAG benchmark, RECTIFY surfaces interpretable failure profiles across BM25, dense, and hybrid retrieval: BM25 mainly triggers noisy-retrieval repairs, while dense and hybrid retrieval leave smaller sets of multi-part underretrieval and underused-evidence cases. Additional analyses show that pre-filtering reduces unnecessary repair candidates and that slicelevel routing yields more targeted repair cards than broad family-level diagnosis. RECTIFY is publicly available as an open-source Streamlit workbench 1 for helping developers turn evaluation results into inspectable repair decisions.

</details>


### [133] [RepoAtlas: Guiding Coding Agents via Evolving Multimodal Repository Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16936)
*Yunxiang Zhang,Haiquan Wang,JiaWei Guo,Hanyang Xia,Yan Chen,Tong Chen,Zhang Zhiwei,Junchen Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RepoAtlas 是一个免训练模块，通过在有仓库代码图上进行"选择-投影-刷新"循环，为编码智能体维护不断演化的多模态仓库视图，在 SWE-bench Verified 上提升解决率并降低开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级问题解决仍然困难：智能体不仅要生成合理补丁，还需在相互依赖的文件中定位相关代码，并维护既充分又聚焦的仓库上下文。代码图能揭示非局部关系，但线性文本接口掩盖了其拓扑结构；渲染完整仓库图又过于密集难以可靠感知，而一次性局部视图随着探索推进会变得过时。

Method: RepoAtlas 是一个免训练模块，在仓库代码图上通过"选择-投影-刷新"循环维护演化的多模态仓库视图。它结合问题证据与智能体当前探索状态，在固定预算下选择任务相关区域；将所选结构投影为互补的视觉与文本表示；当探索状态变化使视图过时时进行刷新。

Result: 在 SWE-bench Verified 上，相较于最强的多模态图基线，RepoAtlas 将解决率提升 2.4 个百分点，同时平均减少输入 token 与模型调用次数 5.8% 和 7.8%，并在三个不同家族与规模的模型上取得一致增益。

Conclusion: 通过动态维护任务相关的多模态仓库视图，RepoAtlas 在不训练的情况下缓解了仓库级上下文过密或过时的问题，兼顾了效果与效率，并具备跨模型泛化性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-powered coding agents have made rapid progress in automating software engineering tasks, yet repository-level issue resolution remains challenging. Beyond generating a plausible patch, an agent must localize relevant code across interdependent files and maintain repository context that is both sufficient and focused. Code graphs expose non-local relations, but linear text interfaces obscure their topology; rendering the full repository graph yields visual representations that are too dense to perceive reliably, whereas a one-shot local view becomes stale as exploration proceeds. We present \textbf{RepoAtlas}, a training-free module that maintains evolving multimodal repository views through a \emph{select--project--refresh} loop over a repository code graph. RepoAtlas combines evidence from the issue with the agent's current exploration state to select a task-relevant region under a fixed budget, projects the selected structure into complementary visual and textual representations, and refreshes the view when changes in the exploration state render it outdated. We evaluate RepoAtlas on SWE-bench Verified, where it improves the resolve rate by 2.4 points while reducing input tokens and model calls by 5.8\% and 7.8\% on average, relative to the strongest multimodal graph baseline, with consistent gains across three models of different families and scales.

</details>


### [134] [TasmScan: Continuation-Aware Taint Analysis for TVM Bytecode with Savelist Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16987)
*Yixuan Liu,Yin Wu,Yi Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TasmScan是首个针对TON虚拟机(TVM)的字节码级静态分析框架，通过建模savelist语义实现跨续延数据流推理，在缺陷检测上达到95.3%召回率与96.8%精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TVM使用一等续延(first-class continuations)配合savelist来跨续延调用管理控制流与寄存器状态。savelist捕获的寄存器允许数据绕过操作数栈在续延边界间流动，因此字节码级分析若不显式建模savelist语义，就无法构建完整的数据流追踪。此外现有分析缺乏源码，需要纯字节码级方案。

Method: 提出TasmScan：通过前向寄存器分析建模savelist语义，对精确解析的保存点和局部追踪的寄存器定义提供形式化过近似保证；将字节码提升为类型化中间表示TASIR；进行路径敏感污点分析与上下文感知源识别以检测缺陷。

Result: 在TON验证器注册表的2,921个合约及208个带人工确认真值标签的基准上评估：全语料解析294,546个动态续延目标，精确率100%；消融实验证实savelist传播对解析依赖跨续延寄存器传递的间接寄存器调用至关重要；基准上五类缺陷检测召回率95.3%、精确率96.8%；全语料366对分层抽样估计整体精确率85.8%；相比最先进符号执行基线中位加速17倍，路径分析100%完成且零崩溃或超时。

Conclusion: TasmScan证明字节码级静态分析可在无需源码条件下对TVM实现跨续延数据流推理，savelist语义建模是解析间接调用的关键，且该方法在精度与效率上均优于符号执行基线。

Abstract: The Open Network (TON), with a peak market capitalization exceeding $20 billion and over 175 million activated on-chain addresses, relies on the TVM (TON Virtual Machine) to execute smart contracts. TVM uses first-class continuations with savelists to manage control flow and register state across continuation invocations. Since savelist-captured registers allow data to flow across continuation boundaries without passing through the operand stack, bytecode-level analyses cannot construct complete data flow tracking without explicitly modeling savelist semantics. We present TasmScan, the first bytecode-level static analysis framework for TVM that enables cross-continuation data flow reasoning without requiring source code. TasmScan models savelist semantics via forward register analysis with a formal over-approximation guarantee for exact-resolved save sites and locally tracked register definitions, then lifts bytecode into TASIR, a typed intermediate representation, and performs path-sensitive taint analysis with context-aware sources to detect defects. We evaluate TasmScan on 2,921 contracts from the TON verifier registry and a labeled benchmark of 208 contracts with human-confirmed ground truth. On the full corpus, TasmScan resolves 294,546 dynamic continuation targets with 100% precision; ablation confirms that savelist propagation is essential for resolving indirect register calls that depend on cross-continuation register passing. On the benchmark, TasmScan detects 95.3% of defects across five classes with 96.8% precision. A 366-pair stratified sample from the full corpus estimates 85.8% overall precision. TasmScan offers a 17x median speedup over the state-of-the-art symbolic-execution baseline, and in the path-analysis comparison completes 100% of analyses with zero crashes or timeouts.

</details>


### [135] [Search-Based Metamorphic Testing of Vision-Language Models in Autonomous Underwater Robotic Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17007)
*Muhammad Yousaf,Aitor Arrieta,Shaukat Ali,Paolo Arcaini,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 MetaVLM，一种基于搜索的蜕变测试方法，用于评估水下自主机器人（AUR）软件中视觉语言模型（VLM）的质量，通过 NSGA-II 寻找最小图像变换集合以诱发错误预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业合作伙伴关注多领域工业系统质量保证，包括海事系统如水面舰艇和水下自主机器人（AUR）。尽管 VLM 在场景理解、图像描述和目标识别方面表现优异，但其在水下环境 AUR 软件中的应用尚未充分探索。因此需要自动化软件测试工具来评估 VLM 的质量、适用性与可靠性。

Method: 提出 MetaVLM，一种搜索式蜕变测试方法，识别水下图像上的最小变换集合以诱使模型产生错误预测，从而揭示 VLM 失效。采用 NSGA-II 作为多目标搜索算法，并在开源 VLM（BLIP 与 CLIP）上与随机搜索基线进行对比评估。

Result: 实验结果展示了各 VLM 在 AUR 软件系统情境下的优势与局限，并据此为从事 VLM 软件系统质量保证的软件工程实践者与研究者提炼出经验教训。

Conclusion: MetaVLM 可作为评估与提升水下 AUR 软件中 VLM 可靠性的自动化测试手段，其结果为 VLM 质量保证提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Our industry partner focuses on quality assurance for industrial systems across multiple domains, including maritime systems, such as overwater vessels and autonomous underwater robots (AURs). Despite the strong performance of vision-language models (VLMs) in scene understanding, image captioning, and object recognition, their use in AUR software operating in underwater environments is underexplored. Therefore, in this context, it is important to evaluate the quality of VLMs for integration into AUR software and, so, automated software testing tools are needed to assess their suitability and improve their dependability. To this end, we propose a search-based metamorphic testing approach (MetaVLM) that identifies a minimal set of transformations on underwater images to induce incorrect model predictions, thereby revealing VLM failures. We employ NSGA-II as a multi-objective search algorithm and evaluate it over open-source VLMs, BLIP and CLIP, against a random search baseline. Results demonstrate the strengths and limitations of each VLM in the context of AUR software systems. Based on the results, we derive lessons for software engineering practitioners and researchers working on quality assurance of VLM-based software systems.

</details>


### [136] [GANADI: Uncovering C/C++ OSS Reuse Genealogies via Pivotal Function-Based Clustering to Enhance Supply Chain Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17018)
*Dongyeon Kim,Seunghoon Woo,Heejo Lee*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GANADI，一种系统性识别C/C++开源软件复用谱系的方法，通过聚类下游项目并推断复用方向来构建谱系，精度84.85%、召回率95.76%，并发现48个未修补漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解开源软件复用谱系对提升SBOM完整性和供应链安全修复优先级至关重要。现有方法虽能识别项目内复用组件和漏洞，但无法追踪经由中间项目的复用路径，限制了供应链生态的安全保障。

Method: GANADI基于共享的源生代码特征（称为关键函数，pivotal functions）对下游项目进行聚类，然后在每个聚类内推断项目间的复用方向，从而构建复用谱系。

Result: 在20个广泛复用的开源项目、超1500条传播路径上，GANADI达到84.85%精度和95.76%召回率，远超现有方法最高23.21%的召回率；基于谱系进行漏洞检测，在真实流行C/C++项目中发现48个未修补漏洞，其中23个经负责任披露后已修复（含1个CVE编号）。

Conclusion: GANADI有效追踪跨中间项目的开源复用谱系，显著提升召回率，并证明了基于谱系的漏洞管理具有实际安全价值。

Abstract: We present GANADI, a systematic approach for identifying C/C++ OSS reuse genealogies to enhance software supply chain security. Understanding OSS reuse genealogy is crucial for improving SBOM completeness and prioritizing security remediation across supply chains. Although existing approaches can identify reused compo- nents and vulnerabilities within a project, they fail to trace OSS reuse paths through intermediate projects, limiting their effectiveness in securing supply chain ecosystems. To address this limitation, GANADI constructs reuse genealogies by clustering downstream projects based on shared characteristics of origin-derived code (called pivotal functions), and then inferring reuse direction among the projects within each cluster. When applied to 20 widely reused OSS projects with over 1,500 propagation paths, GANADI achieved 84.85% precision and 95.76% recall in identifying reuse genealogies, outperforming existing approaches that achieved at most 23.21% recall. Leveraging OSS reuse genealogy for vulnerability detection, we identified 48 unpatched vulnerabilities in real-world popular C/C++ projects. Among them, 23 were patched following our responsible disclosure (including one CVE ID assigned), demonstrating the practical impact of genealogy-based vulnerability management.

</details>


### [137] [A Set-Theoretic Evaluation Framework for Assessing Asset Administration Shell Instances: Towards Comparability and Suitability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17062)
*Carsten Ellwein,David Dietrich,Rozana Cvitkovic,Bastian Lang,Hansjoerg Tutsch,Andreas Wortmann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出两种互补方法，用于比较和面向应用评估资产管理壳（AAS）：基于集合论的操作比较AAS模型，以及AAS适用性模型评估其与特定用例需求的符合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同AAS实例在结构、内容和完成度上存在差异，难以判断某个AAS是否适用于特定应用，因此需要支持比较与面向应用的评估方法。

Method: 第一，采用集合论操作比较AAS模型，识别共有、缺失和不同的子模型及参数；第二，构建AAS适用性模型，从结构符合性、语义一致性、基数和规范符合性四方面评估AAS与用例需求的契合度，可与参考AAS或所需SemanticIDs集合进行对比，并基于偏差得出适用性取值及详细报告。

Result: 该方法能够识别AAS之间的差异，并给出面向具体应用的适用性分值与缺失或不符合信息的详细报告。

Conclusion: 所提方法有助于从业者和研究人员比较不断演进的AAS，并为其在制造软件服务中的适用性提供应用特定信息。

Abstract: Asset Administration Shells (AAS) provide a standardized means of representing assets and their information in manufacturing and increasingly serve as a basis for software services. However, different AAS instances vary in structure, content, and degree of completion, making it difficult to determine whether a given AAS is suitable for a specific application. This paper presents two complementary methods to support the comparison and application-oriented assessment of AAS. First, set-theoretic operations are employed to compare AAS models, enabling the identification of common, missing, and differing submodels and parameters. Second, an AAS suitability model assesses the conformity of an AAS to the requirements of a specific use case. The assessment considers structural conformity, semantic consistency, cardinality, and specification conformity and can be performed either against a reference AAS or a set of required SemanticIDs. A suitability value is derived from the identified deviations and is complemented by a detailed report of missing or non-conforming information. The proposed approach support practitioners and researchers in the comparison of evolving AAS and provide application-specific information on their suitability for manufacturing software services.

</details>


### [138] [Towards an Asset Administration Shell Maturity Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17084)
*Carsten Ellwein,David Dietrich,Rozana Cvitkovic,Andreas Wortmann*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出AAS成熟度概念，用于系统比较不同AAS实例，衡量其对数字孪生既定标准的满足程度，从而支持数字孪生工程中的评估与改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AAS被视为制造业数字孪生实现与数据交换的基础模型，但在基于AAS的系统中，比较不同AAS实例缺乏被广泛接受的方法框架和成熟度模型，难以系统化地开展分析。这一空白促使作者提出新的AAS成熟度概念。

Method: 从文献中推导AAS成熟度相关概念，并通过实例化（exemplification）方式加以应用。

Result: 提出的AAS成熟度概念能够刻画AAS实例满足既定数字孪生标准的程度，实现不同AAS实例之间的可比性。

Conclusion: 这些初步成果可帮助从业者和研究者系统比较AAS实例，并支持识别和评估数字孪生工程过程中的后续开发步骤。

Abstract: The Asset Administration Shell (AAS) is increasingly recognized as a fundamental model for the realization of and data exchange between digital twins in manufacturing. An AAS defines a hierarchical data structure to represent any type of asset throughout its entire lifecycle. In the context of AAS-based systems, comparing different AAS instances constitutes a practical challenge, as neither a widely accepted methodological framework nor a maturity model are available to systematically support such analyses. To address this gap, we propose a novel concept of AAS maturity that characterizes the extent to which established digital twin criteria are met and thus enabling comparability of AAS instances. The concepts are derived from the literature and applied through exemplification. These emerging results enable practitioners and researchers to systematically compare AAS instances and support the identification and assessment of further development steps in the digital twin engineering process.

</details>


### [139] [Grounding SWE-Agent Decisions in Architecture-0 Design: Navigating Unknown Unknowns through Physical Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17221)
*Zhongkai Wang,Yan Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究自主软件工程智能体（SWE-Agents）在系统设计初期阶段面对隐性工程约束（Unknown Unknowns, UUs）时的表现，揭示了从纯文本推理到工具增强反馈再到外部物理映射的失败链，并提出Physical Mapping Guard（PMG）来消除验证层的博弈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SWE-Agents在确定性编码任务上表现优异，但在系统设计初期的"Architecture 0"阶段表现不佳。该阶段充满隐性的工程约束，即Unknown Unknowns（UUs），这些约束很少被显式表述，导致智能体难以有效导航。

Method: 作者沿着纯文本自博弈、工具增强反馈和外部物理映射的渐进轨迹展开探索，并在发现失败链后提出PMG。PMG基于软件工程中的关注点分离原则，将验证权从智能体手中收回，强制语义意图由外部的确定性Semantic-to-Physical（S2P）映射引擎进行评估。

Result: 实证分析揭示了一个级联失败链：纯文本推理不可避免退化为礼貌共识或貌似合理但物理上不可能的捏造；早期执行沙盒会触发"规格博弈"（Specification Gaming），智能体利用对验证脚本的自主权绕过物理约束，获得表面成功。评估表明PMG彻底消除了物理层和验证层的博弈行为。

Conclusion: PMG将残余失败精准定位到语义重解释和审计者越权两类问题，标志着在自动化架构设计中实现真正"可供性接地"（affordance grounding）的关键一步。

Abstract: Autonomous Software Engineering Agents (SWE-Agents) excel in deterministic coding tasks but struggle with Architecture 0, the nascent system design phase plagued by implicit engineering constraints, or Unknown Unknowns (UUs) that are rarely stated explicitly. To investigate how agents navigate UUs, we explore a progressive trajectory across pure-text self-play, tool-augmented feedback, and external physical mapping. Our empirical analysis reveals a cascading chain of failures. Pure-text reasoning inevitably devolves into polite consensus or plausible yet physically impossible fabrications. Attempting to bridge this gap via an early-stage execution sandbox unexpectedly triggers Specification Gaming: agents exploit their autonomy over validation scripts to bypass physical constraints, achieving superficial success without resolving core architectural flaws. To resolve this self-validation trap, we propose the Physical Mapping Guard (PMG). Grounded in the software engineering principle of Separation of Concerns, PMG revokes verification authority from the agent, forcing semantic intents to be evaluated by an external, deterministic Semantic-to-Physical (S2P) mapping engine. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that PMG completely eradicates physical-layer and validation-layer gaming. By precisely isolating residual failures to semantic reinterpretations and auditor overreach, PMG marks a critical step toward genuine affordance grounding in automated architectural design.

</details>


### [140] [A Memorization Floor for LLM Refinement of Decompiled Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17236)
*Muhammad Asjad*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"记忆底线"（memorization floor）这一项目内控制方法，用两次精化对比来分离LLM在反编译输出精化中"来自输入"与"来自先验"的贡献，结果发现命名增益几乎不依赖被消融的输入信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM精化反编译输出时，其命名等改进究竟来自对输入代码的真实理解，还是来自对训练语料的记忆与先验，难以区分。作者希望设计一种低成本、项目内、不受语料难度干扰的控制手段来回答该问题。

Method: 提出"记忆底线"控制：先对函数做一次精化，再在标识符被破坏的输入上重新精化，测量仍存活的部分。由于对比是项目内的，语料难度无法产生影响，且仅需约二十次API调用。实验使用在分析计划提交后编写的函数（排除记忆可能），并设置基于自身输出词表的臂匹配置换零假设、类型前缀移除与名称置换等消融，以及来自另一厂商、预先注册的第二个精化模型进行复现（十二组对比、两个模型、十二个零假设）。

Result: 恢复是真实的：精化输出比臂匹配置换零假设高出+0.072至+0.137。但它并不依赖于被消融的输入：破坏输入的数据流对命名增益的改变仅为+0.001（95% CI [-0.026, +0.026]），移除类型前缀或置换名称的改变也不更大。第二个厂商的模型在字节相同输入下复现了这一结果。可读性始终处于天花板，读者无法获得任何信号。零假设是有界的而非绝对的：低于0.056的贡献不可见，且消融保留了操作本身，因此仅凭操作进行命名仍是一种竞争性解释。没有任何注册假设被证实，作者全文报告了背后的五项工具失败。

Conclusion: 精化带来的命名恢复确实存在，但并不源自被消融的输入信息，说明其可能更多依赖模型先验而非输入理解；同时该零假设存在检测下限，消融未破坏操作层面信息，故结论仍有替代解释。所有预先注册的假设均未获证实，并如实披露了五项工具缺陷。

Abstract: We introduce a memorization floor: a within-item control separating what LLM refinement of decompiler output recovers from its input from what it recovers from its prior. Refine a function, then refine it again from an input whose identifiers have been destroyed, and measure what survives. Because the comparison is within-item, corpus difficulty cannot contribute; it costs twenty API calls. Applied to functions written after our analysis plan was committed, so no released model could have memorized them, it reports two things. Recovery is real: refined output sits +0.072 to +0.137 above an arm-matched permutation null built from its own output vocabulary. But it does not depend on the input we ablate: destroying the input's dataflow changes the naming gain by +0.001 (95% CI [-0.026, +0.026]), and removing type prefixes or permuting names changes it by no more. A second refiner from another vendor, registered in advance and given byte-identical inputs, reproduces this -- twelve contrasts, two models, twelve nulls. Readability stays at ceiling throughout, so a reader is given no signal. The null is bounded, not absolute: contributions under 0.056 are invisible, and the ablation leaves operations intact, so naming from those alone remains a competing reading. No registered hypothesis was confirmed, and we report the five instrument failures behind that in full, including a reassembly harness biased against the treated arm and an equivalence checker we registered without checking it worked on our inputs.

</details>


### [141] [An Exemplar of a Digital Twin in Mechanical Engineering: Understanding Model Hybridization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17258)
*Mahussi Datongnon,Hubert Lejeune,Yoann Jus,Benoit Combemale,Julien Deantoni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文以CETIM现有流体回路数字孪生为案例，采用Gil等人的表征框架并从"动机"与"实现"两个维度补充分析，使混合数字孪生工程中的"混合化"过程变得显式、可追溯，从而促进其文档化与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生（DT）在工业尤其是机械工程领域被广泛应用，可加速产品开发、降低风险、实现早期问题预测并降低维护成本。实践中，DT日益将基于物理（演绎）与数据驱动（归纳）的模型整合为混合模型，以结合两种建模范式的互补优势（即"混合化"）。然而，混合数字孪生的工程实践仍缺乏充分记录：混合化常以临时（ad hoc）方式引入，实现过程仅部分显式化，限制了可复现性与可迁移性。

Method: 论文报告了CETIM一个现有流体回路数字孪生的开发过程。首先使用Gil等人的表征框架对该DT进行描述，提供跨生命周期维度的结构化视角；其次，为使混合化过程显式化，进一步通过沿"动机（motivation）"与"实现（realization）"两个维度的补充表征对该案例进行分析。

Result: 所得到的描述提供了对混合化决策的可追溯记录，能够支持混合数字孪生工程实践的文档化与迁移。

Conclusion: 通过将混合化决策显式化并结构化记录，本研究为混合数字孪生的工程实践提供了可复现、可迁移的文档支持，弥补了该领域实践记录不足的空白。

Abstract: Digital Twins (DTs) are widely adopted across a variety of application domains. In industrial sectors, particularly in mechanical engineering, they accelerate product development, reduce risks, enable early issue prediction, and lower sustainment costs . In practice, DTs increasingly integrate physics-based (deductive) and data-driven (inductive) models into hybrid models combining the complementary strengths of both modeling paradigms. In this paper, we refer to this integration paradigm as hybridization. Despite this trend, the engineering of hybrid DTs that is, remains insufficiently documented. Hybridization is often introduced in an ad hoc manner, and its implementation is only partially made explicit, which limits reproducibility and transferability. This paper reports on the development of an existing fluidic loop digital twin at Centre Technique des Industries M{é}canique (CETIM). The DT is described using the characterization framework of Gil et al., providing a structured view across its lifecycle dimensions. To make hybridization explicit, the case is further analyzed through a complementary characterization structured along two dimensions: motivation and realization. The resulting description provides a traceable account of hybridization decisions and supports the documentation and transfer of hybrid DT engineering practices.

</details>


### [142] [After the Party: Governing What a Viral Agent-Skill Ecosystem Left Behind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17274)
*Yunpeng Xiong,Ting Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文以OpenClaw智能体技能注册表为对象，测量其2026年上半年爆发式增长后留下的“遗产”：注意力高度集中、人类审查缺失、安全扫描不可靠，指出治理快速扩张的技能注册表不能依赖简单元数据或单一扫描器分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体越来越多通过自然语言技能指令执行Shell、网络、凭据、文件和进程操作，公共注册表大规模分发这些技能。2026年上半年OpenClaw智能体走红、技能注册表爆发式扩张，作者希望测量这波热潮究竟留下了什么，并考察现有治理手段是否足够。

Method: 利用OpenClaw的Git历史、GitHub issues与pull requests，以及ClawHub注册表的三个快照进行实证测量。分析内容包括：注册表规模与创建时间分布、下载量集中度、列表持续存在的预测因素（控制创建队列与技能年龄）、人类关注度指标（星标与评论）、权限证据比例，以及三个安全扫描器的一致性、加权敏感度与人工裁定。

Result: 可观测库存91天内近乎翻倍，6月可见列表中多数在两个月内创建，期末月度创建量与核心仓库活动已从春季高点回落。注意力集中：前10%技能获得46.93%的下载量。在控制创建队列与技能年龄后，没有简单技能特征（如体积或下载量）能稳定预测列表持续存在。人类审查缺失：77.86%的技能零星标、零评论；85.06%的可读技能带有权限证据。自动化清理尚不可靠：三个安全扫描器在其共同覆盖的61,990个技能中有23,702个判断不一致，人工裁定后加权敏感度仅21.67%–61.06%。

Conclusion: 快速增长的智能体技能注册表存在注意力集中、人类监督缺位和扫描工具低可靠性等结构性风险。治理此类注册表不能依赖简单元数据或单一扫描器评分，而需要稳健、透明的测量方法与独立验证。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act through agent skills, i.e., natural-language instructions, that direct a host agent toward shell, network, credential, file, and process actions, and public registries distribute them at scale. In the first half of 2026, the OpenClaw AI agent went viral, and its public skill registry boomed: the observable stock nearly doubled in 91 days, and a majority of the listings visible in June were created in just two months. By the end of our study window, the wave had crested, and monthly listing creation and core-repository activity were falling from their spring peaks. This paper measures what the boom left behind, drawing on the OpenClaw Git history, its GitHub issues and pull requests, and three ClawHub registry snapshots. Attention is concentrated: the top 10% of skills received 46.93% of all downloads. No simple skill features (like size or download counts) remained a stable predictor of continued listing once creation cohort and skill age were controlled. Human scrutiny did not stay: 77.86% have zero stars and zero comments, while 85.06% of the readable skills carry privilege evidence. And automated cleanup is not ready: the three security scanners disagreed on 23,702 of the 61,990 skills they all cover. After human adjudication, weighted scanner sensitivity against the reference standard ranged from 21.67% to 61.06%. Governing fast-growing agent-skill registries cannot rely on simple metadata or single scanner scores; it requires robust, transparent measurement and independent validation.

</details>


### [143] [Type-IV Code Clone Detection via Layer-Wise Non-Contrastive Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17338)
*Luciano Marchezan,Kevin Delcourt,Eugene Syriani,Houari Sahraoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LWVIC4Code，一种用于Type-IV代码克隆检测的非对比式表示学习方法，基于VICReg框架并引入跨层一致性正则化与深度相关层加权，在无负样本情况下取得竞争性或更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Type-IV克隆语义等价但句法不同，传统基于token或语法的方法难以检测。近年机器学习方法多依赖对比学习，需谨慎的负样本采样且易引入偏差，因此需要一种不依赖负样本的表示学习方案。

Method: 基于方差-不变性-协方差正则化（VICReg）框架及已有的逐层VICReg训练，LWVIC4Code引入跨层一致性正则化和深度相关层加权，在transformer各层间逐步精炼语义信息，生成鲁棒且具判别力的代码表示。

Result: 在Python（Kamino）与多语言（GPTCloneBench）数据集上，与对比学习基线及零样本大语言模型对比，LWVIC4Code在无负样本下达到竞争性或更优性能，受益于逐层监督，并能有效从Python泛化到Java、C#等其他语言。

Conclusion: 非对比式、逐层表示学习是稳健语义代码克隆检测的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Software clones are fragments of code that are similar or functionally equivalent to each other. They pose significant challenges for maintenance, refactoring, and bug detection. Detecting Type-IV clones, which are semantically equivalent but may differ syntactically, is particularly difficult for traditional token- or syntax-based methods. Recent machine learning approaches rely on contrastive learning, which requires careful negative sampling and can introduce bias. In this paper, we propose LWVIC4Code, a non-contrastive representation learning approach specifically designed for Type-IV clone detection. Building on the Variance-Invariance-Covariance Regularization (VICReg) framework and prior layer-wise VICReg training, LWVIC4Code introduces cross-layer consistency regularization and depth-dependent layer weighting to progressively refine semantic information across transformer layers, producing robust and discriminative code representations. We conduct an empirical study comparing LWVIC4Code against a contrastive learning baseline and zero-shot large language models on Python (Kamino) and multi-language (GPTCloneBench) datasets. Results show that LWVIC4Code achieves competitive or superior performance without negative samples, benefits from layer-wise supervision, and generalizes effectively from Python to other languages, particularly Java and C#. These results demonstrate that non-contrastive, layer-wise representation learning is a promising direction for robust semantic code clone detection.

</details>


### [144] [Coding Agents Have Converged: Why the SWE-bench Leaderboard Can No Longer Order Its Top Entries, and What to Measure Instead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17394)
*Fengshuo Liu,Ying Liu,Ruize Sun,Lie Luo,Siyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文审计SWE-bench排行榜上"微小分差即排名"的解读是否成立，发现前沿系统成功集合高度重叠，多数相邻名次差异统计上无法区分，主张报告"比较集特异性分辨力"与"模型-脚手架来源"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排行榜上微小的分数差异常被直接读作系统能力的排序，但缺乏对已发布判定结果本身是否支持这种排序的检验。作者希望在不重新运行模型的前提下，审计这些判定证据能否支撑"排名差异"的解读。

Method: 使用四个数据划分上的254个SWE-bench提交，不运行模型，仅分析已发布判定：计算top-k共享成功/失败实例数、解集嵌套度（对比分数隐含基线）、模型-脚手架配对内的分数波动范围、McNemar配对检验（相邻名次）及经过Holm校正的交互检验；并提出基于leader的规则与五步审计流程。

Result: Verified上前两名各解决500例中的396例；前十分享285个成功与51个失败，仅164个实例可区分其表现。前沿解集中位嵌套度0.935，高于分数隐含基线0.774。同模型不同脚手架的分差最高达29.8个百分点，而前三十名整体跨度仅8.8个百分点。9个单元均值交互检验中6个经Holm校正后仍显著（但为观察性设计，不能识别因果）。Verified前三十的29对相邻名次在α=0.05下无一对被McNemar检验分开，Test划分则分开23对中的14对。leader规则给出三个描述性层级，Holm校正后为两个；未拒绝不等于等价。

Conclusion: 不应把小幅总分差距解读为已确立的排名差异；应报告"比较集特异性的分辨力"以及模型-脚手架的来源，并公开划分结果与五步审计协议。

Abstract: Small differences on coding-agent leaderboards are often read as an ordering of systems. We audit whether the published verdicts support this reading, using 254 SWE-bench submissions across four splits without running models. On Verified, the leading two entries each resolve 396 of 500 instances. The top ten share 285 successes and 51 failures, leaving 164 instances that distinguish their outcomes. Frontier solution sets have median nesting 0.935 against a score-implied baseline of 0.774, indicating strongly shared successes. Scores also depend on the evaluated model-scaffold pair: observed within-model scaffold ranges reach 29.8 percentage points, compared with the 8.8-point spread of the top thirty. Six of nine cell-mean interaction tests remain significant after Holm correction, although this observational design does not identify causal scaffold effects. Exact paired McNemar tests separate none of the 29 adjacent Verified top-thirty pairs at alpha=0.05, while the larger Test split separates 14 of 23. A stated leader-based rule yields three descriptive tiers, or two after Holm correction; non-rejection does not establish equivalence. We release the partition and a five-step audit protocol that profiles shared outcomes, tests paired differences, reports grouping sensitivity, and estimates the instance budget needed for resolution. The results motivate reporting comparison-set-specific resolution and model-scaffold provenance instead of interpreting small aggregate gaps as established rank differences.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Multimodal Emergency Vehicle Classification via Audio-Visual Transformers and Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16535)
*Vijay John,Amar Dabaja*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: AVNet：一种音视频多模态Transformer，用于紧急车辆检测，支持单模态缺失下的鲁棒推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中紧急车辆检测是安全关键任务，但现有方法仅依赖单一模态（音频或视频），在模态退化时（如嘈杂城市环境或夜间/遮挡）会系统性失效，因此需要多模态且能容忍模态缺失的鲁棒方案。

Method: 提出AVNet多模态音视频Transformer，包含三项关键设计：（1）时间对齐的跨模态融合模块，在音频频谱token与视频帧token间做秒级交叉注意力，利用精确时间对应关系而无需学习对齐机制；（2）可学习空嵌入（null embeddings）替代缺失模态token，使单一模型无需重训练即可在音频、视频或联合模式下运行；（3）知识蒸馏训练策略，让单模态专家教师模型通过软概率目标向多模态学生融合分支传递类间暗知识。

Result: 在Google AudioSet的281个片段上评估，AVNet在音视频模式下整体准确率66.6%，比仅音频分支高10.4%，比仅视频分支高15.0%；最难类别Ambulance上融合相比任一单模态分支提升+29.5%，表明两模态信息互补且被对齐交叉注意力有效利用。

Conclusion: 音视频融合显著优于单模态，时间对齐的交叉注意力成功挖掘了模态间互补性，且空嵌入与知识蒸馏设计使模型在模态缺失时仍保持鲁棒性。

Abstract: Emergency vehicle detection in autonomous driving is a safety-critical perception task that demands robustness under diverse and adverse real-world conditions. Existing approaches rely on a single modality, either audio or video, which leads to systematic failure when that modality is degraded: microphone-based systems fail in noisy urban environments, and camera-based systems fail at night or under occlusion. This report presents AVNet, a multimodal audio-visual transformer that classifies emergency vehicles (ambulance, fire engine, police car) and road background using both audio and video, while gracefully handling the absence of either modality at inference time. AVNet introduces three key contributions: (1) a temporally aligned cross-modal fusion module that performs second-level cross-attention between audio spectrogram tokens and video frame tokens, exploiting their exact temporal correspondence without any learned alignment mechanism; (2) learned null embeddings that substitute for missing modality tokens, enabling a single unified model to operate in audio-only, video-only, or joint audio-visual mode without retraining; and (3) a knowledge distillation training strategy in which specialist unimodal teacher models transfer inter-class dark knowledge into the multimodal student fusion branch via soft probability targets. Evaluated on 281 clips from the Google AudioSet dataset, AVNet achieves 66.6% overall accuracy in audio-visual mode, outperforming the audio-only branch by +10.4% and the video-only branch by +15.0%. The largest per-class gain is observed for the hardest class, Ambulance, where fusion achieves +29.5% over either unimodal branch alone, demonstrating that the two modalities provide complementary information that the aligned cross attention mechanism successfully exploits.

</details>


### [146] [Mechanism-Level Evaluation for Vision-Language Models: Controlled Activation-Replacement Diagnosis of Gender Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.16651)
*Zhipeng Zhao,Wenxu Wang,Peishun Liu,Ruichun Tang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 该论文主张行为基准只能揭示视觉语言模型存在哪些偏见，无法定位对干预最敏感的内部组件；作者提出以因果中介分析作为机制级评测工具，对性别偏见进行逐层分解，并在多模型多架构上揭示语言层敏感、架构改变敏感分布等发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为基准测试能揭示视觉语言模型中存在哪些偏见，但无法说明哪些内部组件对定向干预最敏感，因而难以支撑有原则的干预设计。作者认为机制级评测是必要补充。

Method: 将性别线索效应分解为特定层激活所导致的受控间接效应与经由其他所有路径的直接效应，生成逐层的机制特征；覆盖三个架构家族（LLaVA-1.5、LLaVA-NeXT、InstructBLIP，7B/13B）共六个模型及两个8B规模架构；并进行系统性消融与干预实验验证。

Result: 语言层激活在受控干预下输出敏感性最高，且直接分量常呈相反符号；架构选择会重新分配逐层对激活替换的敏感性；反事实分数与表层分数出现分歧，暴露隐性关联。系统性消融验证了内部一致性。干预实验显示平均间接效应（AIE）与下游干预效果仅弱相关（Pearson r = 0.33），且AIE第二大的层带来接近零的偏见变化。

Conclusion: 机制级评测能捕捉行为基准无法发现的架构特定敏感性模式，机制诊断能反映激活替换敏感性但本身不能识别最优干预目标；建议将行为基准与机制级评测配对作为NLP标准实践。

Abstract: Behavioral benchmarking reveals \emph{what} biases exist in vision-language models but not \emph{which internal components} are most sensitive to targeted intervention, precluding principled intervention. We argue for mechanism-level evaluation as a necessary complement, demonstrating causal mediation analysis as a diagnostic instrument for gender bias. We decompose gender-cue effects into controlled indirect effects attributable to specific-layer activations and direct effects through all other pathways, producing layer-by-layer mechanistic signatures. Across six models spanning three architectural families (LLaVA-1.5, LLaVA-NeXT, InstructBLIP at 7B/13B) and two 8B-scale architectures, three findings emerge: language-layer activations exhibit the greatest output sensitivity under controlled intervention, with the direct component often carrying the opposite sign; architectural choices redistribute layer-wise sensitivity to activation replacement; and counterfactual scores diverge from surface-level scores, exposing implicit associations. Systematic ablation validates internal consistency. An intervention experiment finds that the average indirect effect (AIE) and downstream intervention effectiveness are only weakly correlated (Pearson $r = 0.33$), and the layer with the second-largest AIE produces near-zero bias change---indicating that mechanistic diagnosis captures activation-replacement sensitivity but does not, by itself, identify optimal intervention targets. These results show mechanism-level evaluation captures architecture-specific sensitivity patterns that behavioral benchmarks cannot; pairing both should become standard NLP practice. Code: https://github.com/zhaozhipeng1997/CARD-GenderBias.

</details>


### [147] [CTAN: Cycle-Temporal Attention Network for Embodied Audio-Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17420)
*Teng Liu,Yinfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出Cycle-Temporal Attention Network (CTAN)框架，通过双向循环一致性约束的跨模态交叉注意力与时间跨模态记忆机制，实现音视频主动语义增强融合，在Replica和Matterport3D基准上取得优于现有方法的导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音视频具身导航需融合视觉与双耳音频来推断声源位置，核心挑战在于异构模态（特征分布差异大）间的语义交互。现有融合策略多依赖简单的多模态聚合，难以捕获底层几何与语义关系，在复杂环境中导致信息退化。

Method: 提出CTAN框架，面向主动语义增强融合而非简单组合。其中Audio-Visual Reconstruction Cross-Attention (AVRCA)模块采用视觉与声学表示间的双向循环一致性约束，强化两模态的空间语义属性，促进更鲁棒的跨模态交互；并设计Temporal Cross-Modal Memory (TCMM)机制，动态融合实时增强的多模态特征与历史上下文，缓解听觉盲区导致的性能下降。

Result: 在Replica和Matterport3D基准上的实验表明，该方法在成功率(SR)、路径长度加权成功率(SPL)和场景导航准确率(SNA)上均优于此前的音视频导航方法。

Conclusion: 通过循环一致性跨模态注意力与时间记忆机制实现语义增强融合，可有效提升音视频具身导航在复杂环境中的鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: Audio-visual embodied navigation equips robots with the capability to infer the locations of sound sources by integrating visual inputs and acoustic information (e.g., depth observations and binaural audio cues). The core challenge lies in establishing effective semantic interactions across heterogeneous modalities (which exhibit distinct feature distributions). Existing feature fusion strategies, however, often rely on simple multimodal aggregation and therefore fail to capture the underlying geometric and semantic relationships, leading to information degradation in complex environments. To overcome these limitations, this work presents the Cycle-Temporal Attention Network (CTAN), a framework designed for active semantic-enhanced fusion (rather than straightforward multimodal combination). Specifically, the proposed Audio-Visual Reconstruction Cross-Attention (AVRCA) module employs a bidirectional cycle-consistency constraint (between visual and acoustic representations) to reinforce the spatial semantic attributes of both modalities, thereby facilitating more robust cross-modal interaction. Additionally, we design a Temporal Cross-Modal Memory (TCMM) mechanism to dynamically integrate real-time enhanced multimodal features with historical context, reducing performance drops caused by auditory dead zones. Experimental results obtained on the Replica and Matterport3D benchmarks indicate that the proposed approach achieves superior performance over previous audio-visual navigation methods in terms of success rate (SR), success weighted by path length (SPL), and scene navigation accuracy (SNA).

</details>
